We propose two new shuffle protocols that achieve uniform shuffling with communication complexity $O((k+l)n^2m\log m/\log k)$ for an $m$-by-$l$ matrix shared among $n$ parties, where $k\leq m$ is a tunable parameter. The first protocol is concretely efficient, while the second achieves the best-known $O(nml)$ online communication and $O(n)$ rounds. Experiments show significant improvements in online efficiency and total cost over prior work. Our key technical ingredient is a novel permutation sharing technique that represents permutations using smaller permutation matrices, making their application significantly more efficient. The first protocol applies independent secret permutations sequentially, while the second builds on shuffle correlation to achieve optimal online complexity. We further extend shuffle correlation to support guaranteed output delivery with linear online communication, yielding SLIDE, the first protocol to achieve both $O(nml)$ online communication and guaranteed output delivery. Our constructions rely only on basic Shamir secret sharing over any field of size greater than $n$. As shuffling is a fundamental primitive for MPC tasks such as sorting and oblivious data structures, our results enable more efficient and scalable secure computation in practice.

</details>


### [80] [GameWAM: A World Action Model for Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26200)
*Yuncheng Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Weijia Li*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出GameWAM，据我们所知是首个用于原生闭环游戏与GUI控制的World-Action Model，通过并行视觉-动作生成实现对未来与动作的联合预测，并引入块循环控制以支持长时程交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏智能体直接将视觉与任务上下文映射到动作，缺乏显式世界动态建模；而交互式游戏世界模型虽能由动作预测视觉未来，却不能充当任务策略。二者目标分离，缺乏在游戏开放动态环境中的统一模型。

Method: GameWAM通过块因果条件与流匹配并行生成本帧视觉与可执行键盘鼠标轨迹；构建同步游戏与GUI轨迹用于联合训练；针对异构原生控制，在每个动作步预测模式并以模式特定分布与连续动作归一化生成动作；采用块循环控制，仅执行短前缀动作后从新观测重规划，并通过细粒度块内上下文与层级跨块历史维持时间连续性。

Result: 实验表明，GameWAM在任务成功率上与对比智能体相当，但执行的原生动作更少；同时发现低频频谱成分系统性影响生成的粗粒度相机运动，揭示了生成控制中的源敏感性失败模式。

Conclusion: GameWAM首次统一了游戏中的世界建模与闭环策略，验证了联合视觉-动作生成在原生控制下的有效性；LASI现象的发现为未来稳健生成控制提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Modern video games combine first-person perception, rapid visual changes, persistent world state, and heterogeneous native controls. Existing game agents map visual and task context directly to actions but lack explicit world dynamics modeling, whereas interactive game world models predict visual futures from supplied actions but do not serve as task policies. World-Action Models (WAMs) unify these objectives, but remain largely unexplored under the dynamics and open-ended interaction of video games. We introduce GameWAM, to our knowledge the first WAM for native closed-loop gameplay and GUI control. GameWAM jointly generates future visual observations and executable keyboard-mouse trajectories through parallel visual and action generative processes with block-causal conditioning and flow matching. To support joint world-action learning, we construct synchronized gameplay and GUI trajectories. To handle heterogeneous native control, GameWAM predicts a gameplay/GUI mode at each action step and generates actions with mode-specific prediction distributions and continuous-action normalization. For long-horizon interaction, block-cycle control predicts beyond the committed horizon, executes only a short action prefix, and replans from new observations, while fine-grained within-cycle context and hierarchical cross-cycle history preserve temporal continuity. Experiments demonstrate competitive task success with fewer executed native actions than the compared agents. We further uncover Low-Frequency Action Source Imprinting (LASI), in which low-frequency components of the sampled action source systematically steer coarse generated camera motion under fixed conditioning, revealing a source-sensitivity failure mode in generative control. Project page is available at https://yunncheng.github.io/GameWAM/.

</details>


### [81] [Same Model, Different Harness: Different Coding-Agent Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26218)
*Sydney Lewis*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在固定模型和任务的情况下，改变编码智能体的外部框架（harness）会显著影响性能，尤其在上下文紧张时，压缩旧工具结果和响应停滞的机制能提升修复率与完整解数量，因此评估应把模型与框架视为整体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体评估常聚焦模型本身，但实际系统由模型与harness共同构成。harness控制信息流和工具使用方式，可能独立影响结果。本文旨在验证在模型和任务不变时，仅改变harness配置是否会导致性能差异，从而强调联合评估的必要性。

Method: 对比同一harness的两种配置：控制组按时间顺序提供完整对话；处理组在上下文紧张时机械缩短旧工具结果，并对重复或停滞工作做出响应。在三个编码基准（SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Pro、FeatureBench）上，设置紧窗口（20,480 token）和宽窗口条件，固定任务与尝试时间（480秒），并额外用三个不同模型验证处理效果的普适性。

Result: 紧窗口下处理组在三个压力比较中均提高平均每任务F2PF，并在SWE-bench Verified和Pro上增加完整解数量。以Verified 169任务为例，F2PF从28%升至49%，完整解从43%升至72%。无需针对模型调整，同一处理对另外三个模型同样有效。宽窗口下Qwen3.6在Verified和Pro上两组接近，但FeatureBench处理组F2PF仍较高；处理组每轮提示token更少。

Conclusion: harness的改动能在不改变模型权重的情况下改变可完成的成果，说明编码智能体的性能是模型与harness协同作用的结果。评估应把二者作为整体看待，而非单独评价模型。

Abstract: A coding agent combines a model with a harness, which decides what the model sees, which tools it can use, and how the work continues. We ask whether changing the harness changes the result when the model and task stay fixed. We compare two configurations of the same harness on three coding benchmarks. The control supplies the full conversation in time order, while the treatment keeps the same record but mechanically shortens older tool results as the context fills and responds to repeated or stalled work.
  Under tight context, the treatment raises mean per-task fail-to-pass fraction (F2PF) in all three pressure comparisons and increases complete solutions on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro. The tight-window Verified comparison uses 169 tasks, a 20,480-token window, and a fixed 480-second attempt endpoint; on this cohort, treatment raises mean per-task F2PF from 28 percent to 49 percent and complete solutions from 43 to 72. Without model-specific retuning, the same frozen treatment also raises both endpoints on the same cohort for three additional models with different designs. In the wide-window Qwen3.6 comparisons, observed arm outcomes are close on Verified and Pro, while FeatureBench retains a higher mean per-task F2PF under treatment. On the wide-window Verified cohort, treatment also serves fewer prompt tokens per turn. Because changing the harness changed what unchanged model weights could accomplish, coding-agent evaluations should treat the model and harness together as the tested solver.

</details>


### [82] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出SimCast-S2S，一种基于潜空间扩散模型的次季节到季节降水概率预报框架，通过变分自编码器压缩计算空间、LoRA迁移学习应对数据稀缺，在再分析数据上超越深度学习基线，并与ECMWF-S2S等业务系统表现相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S降水预报对金融和社会影响重大，但预测信号弱、不确定性高且业务系统计算成本高昂；现有数据驱动方法多限于确定性预报，缺乏不确定性量化能力，且扩散模型在物理空间生成大集合成本高、训练数据需求大。

Method: 采用生成式潜扩散模型：用变分自编码器学习紧凑潜空间以高效生成大集合；通过LoRA迁移学习先在气候模拟大数据上预训练，再在有限再分析数据上微调；最终从潜空间采样实现概率预报。

Result: 在再分析数据上优于CNN和U-Net等深度学习基线；尽管只使用部分大气输入变量且无后处理、偏差校正或标定，仍与ECMWF-S2S等业务系统竞争力相当，且在多数情况下表现更好。

Conclusion: 潜空间生成建模结合模拟到再分析的迁移学习，为数据驱动的S2S概率降水预报提供了高效、可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [83] [Neural Regression with Embeddings for Numerical Attribute Prediction in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26729)
*Rupesh Sapkota,Louis Mozart Kamdem Teyou,Moshood Yekini,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出 LitEm 神经回归模型，使直推式知识图谱嵌入模型能够预测数值属性，并设计共训练框架提升链接预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入模型忽略数值属性，限制了对含丰富数值信息真实知识图谱的表示能力。

Method: LitEm 是神经回归模型，可与直推式嵌入模型结合；进一步提出共训练框架，使嵌入模型与 LitEm 联合训练，同时预测链接和数值属性。

Result: 在 FB15K-237、YAGO15K、DB15K 和 Mutagenesis 上多数属性取得最优或次优；共训练主要提升双线性模型的链接预测，并增强属性编码能力。

Conclusion: 共训练不仅提升性能，还使模型以字面感知方式编码属性，增益非来自参数增加；代码已公开。

Abstract: In recent years, transductive knowledge graph embedding models have been applied to tasks such as link prediction and query answering. Although knowledge graphs often contain rich numerical attributes, most embedding models neglect them, limiting their ability to represent real-world knowledge graphs with diverse information. In this work, we propose a neural regression model (LitEm) that enables transductive knowledge graph embedding models to predict numerical attributes within knowledge graphs. Experimental results demonstrate that LitEm achieves the best or second-best results on most attributes across FB15K-237, YAGO15K, DB15K, and Mutagenesis. Furthermore, we propose a co-training framework that jointly trains state-of-the-art transductive knowledge graph embedding models with LitEm, which improves link prediction performance mainly for bilinear models and simultaneously enables them to predict numerical attributes. In addition, the literal-awareness evaluation demonstrates that co-training helps models to encode and exploit attribute information in a "literal-aware'' manner, suggesting that the observed gains are not merely due to additional parameters. We publicly release our implementation at https://github.com/dice-group/dice-embeddings.

</details>


### [84] [Self-Augmented Diffusion Guidance for Physics-Informed Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26748)
*Akira Osaka,Naoya Takeishi,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散引导与自生成数据增强的物理信息扩散模型，通过条件生成实现物理约束，无需在采样中反复求解控制方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准扩散模型生成流体动力学等时空信号时不考虑物理定律，导致样本视觉合理但动态偏差大。

Method: 学习以物理偏差程度为条件的数据分布，在生成时显式设定偏差为零；将控制方程评估与扩散训练/采样解耦，避免迭代求解。

Result: 相比标准扩散模型显著降低偏差，且与现有物理约束扩散方法结合可进一步减少偏差。

Conclusion: 该方法高效且通用，适用于计算昂贵的数值模拟场景，并能与现有方法互补提升物理一致性。

Abstract: Diffusion models can be used to generate spatiotemporal signals of physical phenomena, such as time-series images of fluid dynamics. However, a major limitation of standard diffusion models is that they do not incorporate constraints derived from the underlying physical laws. Consequently, generated samples may appear visually plausible while deviating substantially from the true dynamics. In this study, we propose a simple yet effective physics-informed approach based on diffusion guidance with self-generated data augmentation. The proposed method learns the data distribution conditioned on the degree of deviation from the physically correct dynamics and generates samples by explicitly setting the deviation condition to be zero. The method decouples the evaluation of the governing equations from the diffusion model training and sampling processes, avoiding the need to solve the governing equations at every iteration of the denoising process. This design makes the method applicable to problems requiring computationally expensive numerical simulations and enables faster sample generation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model not only significantly reduces the deviations compared with standard diffusion models but also achieves further reductions when combined with existing physics-constrained diffusion methods.

</details>


### [85] [When Is the Sharp Covariance Envelope Tight? Feature-Only Geometry for Volume-Sampled Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26877)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个关于普通索引固定大小体积采样后接选择未加权最小二乘估计的系数协方差矩阵的Loewner包络，并分析了其全局尖锐性、可达性条件及特征谱相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的分析主要针对端点s=d的情形，本文旨在填补任意预算d<=s<=m下的空白，并区分全局尖锐性与实际池上的可达性，为特征选择提供理论依据。

Method: 利用特征边际nu_A定义精确的固定设计谱相位，通过残差增广的测度变换导出响应感知机制和单侧定量松弛界，并用支撑饱和证明可达方向，辅以关键等杠杆几何解释边界。

Result: 证明了当nu_A>0时谱包络严格，nu_A=0时存在谱紧致残差；给出了保守的基元基数决策证书，并通过冻结特征示例验证证书非空且量化了简化代价。

Conclusion: 所有结论限定于条件中心化的全Gram白化系数协方差，不涉及总体泛化；该方法为固定池下的子集选择提供了可操作的判断准则。

Abstract: Prior analyses by Derezinski and Warmuth established all-size sampling identities, selected-OLS unbiasedness, and inverse moments for ordinary volume sampling, while their exact arbitrary-fixed-response loss and prediction-covariance formulas are at the rank-size endpoint s=d. We establish a Loewner envelope for centered coefficient covariance for every full-rank fixed pool, response, and legal budget d <= s <= m under ordinary indexed fixed-size volume sampling followed by selected unweighted least squares; its coefficient is globally sharp over the full-rank class. Global sharpness does not determine attainability on the pool in hand. Under positive loss, strict-interior budgets, and no coloops, a feature-only margin nu_A gives the exact fixed-design spectral phase: nu_A > 0 if and only if the normalized spectral envelope is strict for every compatible residual, whereas nu_A = 0 if and only if some compatible residual is spectrally tight; the same zero-margin residual is tight at every strict-interior budget. A residual-augmented change of measure supplies the response-aware mechanism and a one-sided quantitative slack bound, while support saturation proves the attainment direction. Critical equal-leverage geometry interprets the boundary, and sound lower certificates yield conservative same-primitive cardinality decisions. Frozen-feature examples show that the certificate is nonvacuous and measure the fixed-pool cost of its authorized reduction. The claims concern conditional centered, full-Gram-whitened coefficient covariance, not population generalization.

</details>


### [86] [Gromov-Monge Flow Matching for Equivariant Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26961)
*Moritz Piening,Christian Wald*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出基于图商空间的Gromov-Monge耦合用于流匹配，通过节点对齐改善图生成质量，尤其在少步采样时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图在节点置换下不变，但现有流匹配未充分利用源-目标耦合中的对称性，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 理论证明商耦合可无损提升为对齐表示，并对称化得到等变流匹配最小化器；实践中用Gromov-Wasserstein松弛和下界构造小批量耦合，结合内外层分配。

Result: 连续图与分子生成任务中，结构感知耦合显著提升少步采样质量，扩展到分子模型在常规多步采样下仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 仅修改训练耦合即可兼容标准等变架构，为图生成提供有效且即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Graphs are invariant under node permutations, motivating the use of permutation-equivariant architectures in generative models. In flow matching, however, symmetry may also enter the source--target coupling: once graph pairs are compared up to node relabeling, the natural Wasserstein geometry is that of the graph quotient space. The Euclidean quotient metric of this space coincides with the Gromov--Monge distance, obtained by optimally relabeling the nodes. We develop this perspective theoretically, showing that quotient couplings can be lifted to aligned representatives without additional cost and that symmetrization yields equivariant flow-matching minimizers, including for categorical endpoint prediction. In practice, exact Gromov--Monge alignment is intractable, so we construct minibatch couplings using efficient Gromov--Wasserstein-type relaxations and lower bounds for the inner node alignment, optionally combined with an outer assignment between graphs. The resulting procedure changes only the training coupling and is compatible with standard permutation-equivariant architectures. Across continuous graph and categorical molecular generation, these structure-aware couplings substantially improve sample quality at small integration budgets, while our scaled-up molecular models remain competitive under conventional many-step sampling.

</details>


### [87] [ClusterAttention: A training-free speedup of bidirectional attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26965)
*Kasper Nordenram,Amelie Dittmann*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ClusterAttention，一种无需训练的双向注意力加速方法，通过快速递归聚类生成固定大小的块稀疏注意力，并在排除簇时用质心补偿误差，在表格数据和视频生成任务上实现显著加速且保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法依赖输入结构或需多次前向传播的慢聚类，无法高效处理无结构输入和单次前向传播场景。

Method: 使用快速递归聚类适应每头键和查询的几何分布，将簇大小设为2的幂以匹配GPU块稀疏计算；推导输出误差表达式，并通过质心补偿被排除簇来减小误差。

Result: 在TabPFN-3上加速2-6倍且保持99%精度；在Wan 2.1-14B视频生成中加速1.8倍，优于SVOO的1.4倍，且输出更接近密集注意力。

Conclusion: ClusterAttention是首个适用于无结构输入和单次前向传播的免训练加速方法，通过质心补偿有效平衡速度与精度。

Abstract: This paper introduces ClusterAttention, a general training-free speedup of bidirectional attention layers. Existing sparse attention methods either rely on structure in the input, such as order in language or spatial proximity in images, or use slow clustering processes amortized over several forward passes. ClusterAttention instead uses a fast recursive clustering method that adapts to the geometry of the keys and queries in each attention head to produce useful clusters. This method allows setting the size of the clusters arbitrarily. We utilize this by setting all clusters to be a fixed size that is a power of two, allowing the block-sparse attention to run at the same latency per query-key interaction as dense attention on GPUs. We also derive an expression for the output error in sparse attention, that explains the counterintuitive experimental finding that tight clusters can lead to larger errors than random clusters. We then derive the error when excluded clusters are compensated through their centroids, and show that this error shrinks with tighter clusters. We integrate this compensation into the method.
  On large-scale tabular data ClusterAttention speeds up TabPFN-3 arXiv:2605.13986 by two to six times, while retaining at least 99% of the dense accuracy. To our knowledge, it is the first training-free method that can be successfully applied in the setting of unstructured input and a single forward pass. For video generation with Wan 2.1-14B T2V arXiv:2503.20314 , ClusterAttention achieves output closer to dense attention and a larger speedup (1.8x versus 1.4x) compared to SVOO arXiv:2603.18636 , a leading method developed specifically for this domain, both run without offline calibration.

</details>


### [88] [Discovering Relationships in Data Lakes Using Large Language Models: An Industrial Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26750)
*Ahlame Diouan,Eric Ferey,Sabine Loudcher,Jérôme Darmont*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出ColRel两阶段方法，利用元数据和数据构建列嵌入，结合业务词典解决ERP数据集中编码/缩写模式标签的列关系发现难题，在弱信号语义场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖依赖元数据保持可用性，但现有元数据常有限且对列关系发现信息弱，尤其ERP派生数据集的模式标签常为编码或缩写形式，导致传统方法难以发现列间语义关联。

Method: ColRel两阶段方法：第一阶段从摄入时的元数据和数据构建列嵌入，对编码模式利用业务词典解释列名并生成简短自然语言描述；第二阶段基于这些描述进行列关系发现。

Result: 在公开基准和工业ERP数据集上实验，ColRel在语义相关、弱信号场景下尤其有效。

Conclusion: ColRel在元数据受限及编码模式的复杂数据集上，能显著提升列关系发现的准确性和实用性，适合工业数据湖场景。

Abstract: Data lakes rely on metadata to remain usable, yet this meta data is often limited or weakly informative for column relationship discovery, especially in ERP-derived datasets with coded or abbreviated schema labels. We propose ColRel, a two-stage method that builds column embeddings from metadata and data available at ingestion time. In difficult cases, such as coded schemata, business dictionaries help better interpret column names and support the generation of short natural-language descriptions used in the second stage. Experiments on public benchmarks and an industrial ERP dataset show that ColRel is particularly effective in semantically related, weak-signal settings.

</details>


### [89] [Benchmarking_Fast_Domain_Adaptation_for_Unsupervised_Speech_Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26992)
*Robin San Roman,Manel Khentout,Tu Anh Nguyen,Paul Michel,Yossi Adi,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于AESRC数据集的ABX-Accent基准，用于评估语音表示学习在带口音英语上的领域适应性，并展示了一种自适应域归一化方法，在跨说话人ABX指标上平均相对提升23.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法在下游任务上表现良好，但对域外语音（尤其是长尾口音）的适应性和少样本适应能力缺乏研究，需要专门的基准和适应方法。

Method: 构建ABX-Accent基准，包含10种英语口音的小规模无标签训练集，并扩展Zero Resources Challenge的ABX评估指标；提出基于自适应域归一化的方法，对预训练Contrastive Predictive Coding模型进行口音微调，先在LibriSpeech的男女划分上开发，再迁移到新基准。

Result: 相比未适应模型，所提方法在跨说话人ABX分数上平均相对提升23.6%。

Conclusion: 该基准和适应方法能有效提升口音多样场景下的表示学习性能，且数据和指标将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Representation learning has attracted great atten- tion and managed to reach good performances as a pretraining method for downstream tasks or as a first step towards unsu- pervised speech modeling. Yet, little is known about how such methods deal with out-of-domain speech and how could they be adapted in a few shot to new domains. This is important especially for accented speech where one observes a long tail of accents that diverge from the standard ones. We introduce ABX- Accent, a benchmark based on the AESRC dataset that features 10 different accents of English. It includes a small (< 10 hours) unlabelled training set in each of the accents and adaptations of the Zero Resources Challenge ABX evaluation metrics to each of the accents. We illustrate this benchmark with a baseline model that uses adaptive domain normalization to fine tune a pretrained Contrastive Predictive Coding model on the accents. This method is first developed on LibriSpeech using a male/female split. When applied to the new benchmark, the proposed method yields a relative improvement of 23.6% on across-speaker ABX scores on average compared to non adapted models. The data and metrics will be open sourced upon paper acceptance

</details>


### [90] [Disentangling Optimization Scale from Preference Scale in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27032)
*Ivan Kruzhilov*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文发现DPO中的β系数纠缠了偏好噪声尺度和优化动力学两个作用，导致策略偏离非单调、损失不可比，并提出centered-softplus重构以解耦二者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO目标中β被简单视为KL约束系数，但实际上它同时影响偏好噪声尺度和有效步长，造成超参数敏感和调度困难，需要解耦以改善可解释性和可控性。

Method: 提出centered-softplus重新参数化DPO损失，保持argmin等价性，同时将逆偏好噪声尺度和学习率效应显式分离、独立调节，并给出β→0的连续极限退化为线性边际目标。

Result: 理论分析表明固定学习率下策略偏差随β非单调（小β死区、中β峰值、大β下降），且相同损失曲线下KL可差数倍；新目标克服上述问题并支持连续端点。

Conclusion: centered-softplus目标解耦β的双重作用，提升DPO的可控性与可解释性，并为偏好学习提供更稳定的优化基础。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used objective for aligning language models from preference data, with the coefficient $β$ commonly interpreted as controlling the KL constraint to a reference policy. We show that $β$ entangles two distinct roles: it governs the effective inverse preference-noise scale and simultaneously rescales the optimization dynamics, coupling this scale with the effective step size. As a consequence, at a fixed learning rate the achieved policy deviation is non-monotone in $β$: it vanishes in a dead zone at small $β$, reaches a peak at an intermediate value, and decreases again for larger $β$. Moreover, standard DPO loss values are not comparable across $β$: runs with nearly identical loss curves can differ several-fold in KL divergence from the reference model. This entanglement obscures the role of $β$, increases sensitivity to hyperparameter choices, and complicates learning-rate scheduling. We propose a centered-softplus reformulation that is argmin-equivalent to DPO for $β>0$, while making the inverse preference-noise-scale and learning-rate effects explicit and independently tunable. The normalized centered-softplus objective also admits a continuous $β\to0$ endpoint that reduces to a linear preference-margin objective.

</details>


### [91] [Unifying Detection and Adaptation in Task-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27070)
*Dezheng Han,Anbang Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Fisher引导的统一框架FiUni，通过K-FAC近似Fisher信息主子空间的正交性检测批次任务，并结合LoRA动态扩展子空间，实现无需任务边界的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续学习方法依赖显式任务边界，难以应对现实任务流场景，需探索无任务边界下的自适应任务检测与参数高效更新机制。

Method: 利用预训练模型Fisher信息矩阵的K-FAC近似主子空间正交性度量任务相似性；为每个输入批次匹配历史子空间，决定复用、扩展或新建低秩适应子空间，实现动态知识共享与隔离。

Result: 能有效推断潜在批次任务归属，在多个基准上以更少可训练参数达到与任务感知方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: FiUni为无任务边界的LLM持续学习提供了一种高效且可扩展的方案，兼顾参数效率与任务隔离，并拓展Fisher信息在任务检测中的应用。

Abstract: To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in downstream continual learning (CL) for large language models (LLMs), existing methods typically constrain parameter updates or introduce task-specific adaptation modules. However, these methods often rely on explicit task boundaries during training, limiting their applicability to realistic task-free scenarios. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{Fi}sher-guided \textbf{uni}fied (\textbf{FiUni}) framework for batch-level task detection and parameter-efficient continual adaptation. FiUni is motivated by a key observation about the Fisher information matrix (FIM) of pre-trained models: the orthogonality among the principal subspaces of its Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) approximation, estimated from a small number of downstream task samples, can reflect the similarity between different tasks. Based on this observation, FiUni constructs FIM-derived frozen subspaces to guide low-rank adaptation (LoRA), while matching the Fisher principal subspace of each incoming batch window with historical subspaces. This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [92] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种由高层目标自主生成的情感偏好调节机制，通过多目标强化学习框架实现低层目标间相对优先级的动态调整，并验证其在复杂环境中的有效性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策框架通常需要外部预设各目标的优先级，但真实环境中目标和内部状态不断变化，需要由高层目标自发产生情绪性偏好以灵活调节低层目标的相对重要性，从而提升决策的适应性与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建双层结构：内层为多目标强化学习控制器，提供可条件化的目标导向行为库；外层为偏好生成器，通过强化学习学习从当前状态到目标偏好向量的映射，将情绪化偏好定义为状态依赖的动态优先级调节，并分析策略空间及最优性差距上界。

Result: 在自建多目标探索环境中，所学偏好函数展现出上下文相关的优先级切换、梯度式权衡和时间持续性，性能优于固定偏好与手工设计偏好策略。

Conclusion: 情感化偏好可由高层目标通过强化学习自主涌现，而非外部指定，为智能体在动态环境下灵活调节多目标优先级提供了可行计算框架。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [93] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文研究电力市场中基于学习的智能体投标策略可能引发默契合谋的问题，通过多智能体强化学习建模并设计多维评估标准，实验证明在寡头垄断和重复博弈条件下，智能体仅通过独立学习即可形成超竞争结果，但须区分理性策略与默契合谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场参与者日益采用学习算法，而其他算法市场已有证据表明纯粹独立学习可能产生默契合谋；加之电力市场寡头垄断且重复互动频繁，结构上易受非竞争行为影响，因此需检验这一假设的可行性。

Method: 将战略性投标建模为具有不完美公共监测的重复博弈，采用多智能体强化学习模拟参与者行为，并提出超越利润对比纳什均衡的多维标准（如价格水平、响应模式、惩罚机制等）以识别默契合谋。

Result: 实验发现部分场景中智能体在未被指示合谋的情况下，确实学会维持支持默契合谋指标的超竞争结果，证实该风险在电力市场中现实存在。

Conclusion: 电力市场存在通过独立学习产生默契合谋的真实危险，监管者需警惕算法化市场的竞争性风险，并发展相应的检测与干预工具。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [94] [Counterfactual Bias Testing for Application Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26899)
*Sai Yashwant,Shruti Bansal,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Satyam Kumar,Shreyash Gupta,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM代理的自动化候选人-职位匹配审计方法，生成合成简历注入人口统计特征，计算九项公平性指标并输出PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL报告，实验发现单一分数或保留率指标会遗漏边界问题，强调多指标多族审计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对应审计研究依赖手工简历和人工提交，成本高且无法跟上模型快速迭代；新兴法规（如EU AI Act）将此类系统列为高风险，亟需可扩展、低成本的偏见审计方法。

Method: 使用任务专用LLM代理合成身份中立简历并注入五种受保护特征（性别/年龄/居住地/语言/残疾），构建K×(1+N)对应审计矩阵；按EU AI Act对齐提示定性标记推断特征；用微调句嵌入模型和余弦相似度排名；计算覆盖反事实、群体公平性和能力感知的九指标套件，含bootstrap置信区间、显著性检验和Benjamini-Hochberg校正，最终生成自动报告和复合风险评分。

Result: 在5个职位、100个候选人、10个处理（90个指标变体）的示例语料上，分数偏移、top-K保留和能力感知差距均在容忍范围内，但排名稳定性指标MARC和nDCG@K各自发现边界问题（包括中性基线本身），单一分数或保留率视图会遗漏这些发现。

Conclusion: 多指标、多族审计优于任何单一聚合分数；LLM代理生成审计可作为人工审计的实用低成本补充，适用于任何候选人-职位匹配流水线。

Abstract: Automated candidate-job matching systems are increasingly classified as high-risk AI under emerging regulation, yet auditing them for demographic bias is expensive: classical correspondence-audit studies require hand-crafted resumes and manual submission, which does not scale to fast pipeline retraining cycles. This paper presents a general, reusable methodology that (1) uses task-specialized LLM agents to synthesize identity-neutral base resumes and inject controlled demographic treatments across five protected-characteristic axes (sex/gender, age, residence, language, disability), producing a K x (1+N) correspondence-audit matrix; (2) qualitatively flags inferred protected characteristics per an EU AI Act-aligned prompt; (3) ranks candidates against a job description via a fine-tuned sentence-embedding model and cosine similarity; and (4) computes a nine-metric fairness suite spanning counterfactual (score delta, mean absolute rank change, flip rate), group-fairness (top-K retention, four-fifths/impact ratio), and merit-aware (Recall@K, nDCG@K, equal opportunity, equalized odds) families, each with bootstrap confidence intervals, significance tests, and Benjamini-Hochberg correction, culminating in an automated PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL report with a composite risk score. On an example corpus of 5 job orders, 100 base candidates, and 10 demographic treatments (90 metric x variant evaluations): score shifts, top-K retention, and merit-aware rate gaps stay within tolerance for every treatment, but a rank-stability metric (MARC) and nDCG@K each surface borderline findings - including one on the neutral baseline itself - that a score- or retention-only view would miss. The results argue for multi-metric, multi-family auditing over any single aggregate score, and for LLM-agent-generated audits as a practical, low-cost complement to human-curated audits for any candidate-job matching pipeline.

</details>


### [95] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 结构化支持向量机的Fisher一致性条件‘损失为度量且满足公共测地线性质’对坐标式argmax解码器不充分，并给出最小反例（四输出星图）及树度量上的充要分类（路径树一致，分支树仅在边界分布失效），同时构造全支撑反例（K_{2,3}，三维Hamming立方体），揭示解码器间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究给出结构化SVM Fisher一致性的必要条件是任务损失为满足‘任意三输出存在公共测地线’的度量，但该条件是否充分未知。本文旨在检验该条件对常用坐标式argmax解码器是否真正保证一致性，并精确刻画满足条件的度量空间中一致性成立的边界。

Method: 通过构造精确的原始-对偶最优性证书，分析不同度量空间（星图、树、完全二分图、Hamming立方体）上argmax解码器的Bayes一致性；利用线性规划对偶和全支撑/边界分布的分类技术，给出最小反例与充要条件。

Result: 四输出单位星图存在最优化得分向量使所有argmax都非Bayes，证明原条件不充分；正权树度量中仅路径树满足argmax一致性，分支树仅在边界分布失效；全支撑反例最小需要5个输出（K_{2,3}），且三维Hamming立方体也有全支撑反例；所有结论均有精确证书。

Conclusion: 在结构化SVM的polyhedral设置中，公共测地线条件只能保证存在某个校准链接，但无法保证预设的argmax链接在所有代理风险最小化器上有效，存在具体的解码器间隙；完全分类有助于实际选择可一致解码的输出空间结构。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [96] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 机器学习按利润而非预测精度评分，作为标准氮建议的修正项可降低利润损失，而非替代标准建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准氮肥建议未响应价格波动，而机器学习常以预测精度评判，但高精度并不直接转化为更高利润，需直接按利润损失评估建议。

Method: 基于892条产量响应曲线构建测试台，扫描氮/粮价格比覆盖所有价格情景，对比机器学习模型与标准建议的利润表现，并测试简单修正步骤及混合策略。

Result: 机器学习预测精度差，无法达到农场容忍度；正常价格下利润不如标准建议。简单修正可削减25%利润损失，且无重训练时在第二地点削减43%；混合策略消除偏差并减少罕见大损失。

Conclusion: 机器学习应作为标准建议的利润导向修正项，而非完全替代；该框架还可为减排成本定价，成本与当前碳价相当。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [97] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出HALO，一个领域特定的IMU基础模型，通过两阶段训练（异构感知自监督预训练+同义词感知软对比学习）解决传感异构性和开放集泛化问题，在7个留出数据集上全面超越5个SOTA基线，零样本开放集精度提升13.7个百分点，且参数量仅为最新模型的1/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR模型缺乏跨主体、设备、活动的统一泛化能力，面临采样率/通道配置/传感器位置异构和未见活动类别两大挑战，且多数方法依赖每数据集分类器，难以实现开放集识别。

Method: 两阶段框架。阶段1：异构感知自监督预训练，含自适应池化token化、通道独立特征提取、上下文传感器条件注入（自然语言描述嵌入每个通道）；阶段2：通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需数据集特定分类器。

Result: 在10个训练集+7个留出集上，HALO在全部8个聚合指标上超越5个SOTA基线；在基线匹配输入的4个设置中仍保持3个领先；参数量约35M（MOMENT的1/10），87个训练标签上的零样本开放集精度提升13.7个百分点；但在两个严重分布偏移数据集上所有模型均失效。

Conclusion: HALO证明结合异构感知预训练和语言对齐能有效提升IMU基础模型的开放集泛化能力，且参数量更低、零样本性能更强；但对极端分布偏移仍脆弱，需进一步研究域适应或鲁棒特征学习。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [98] [Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27044)
*Wei-Yao Wang,Kazuya Tateishi,Shuyang Cui,Christian Simon,Takashi Shibuya,Shusuke Takahashi,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出首个全模态交互通用嵌入模型OmniUE，统一文本、视频、音频的嵌入空间，并支持文本、视觉区域和音频片段等多模态交互查询。在多个基准上超越SOTA，平均提升10.5%至83.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型主要关注语言和图像，交互方式受限，缺乏对视频、音频及多种交互形式的统一支持，无法满足用户多样化的条件查询需求。

Method: OmniUE利用可学习token的中间层表示构建跨文本、视频和音频的统一嵌入空间；引入视觉和音频分割器处理用户交互，结合全模态LLM进行上下文聚合，生成用户条件化的任意到任意嵌入。

Result: 在文本交互视频基准（MMEB-v2-video）上平均提升10.5%，音频任务（MAEB）提升1.1%，视觉交互基准（SCaR）提升83.7%，全模态交互基准（OmniCHOIR）提升24.1%。

Conclusion: 同时推进全模态表示学习和全模态交互查询是迈向通用嵌入模型的关键方向，OmniUE验证了该路线的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has been shifting from traditional two-tower architectures to large language model (LLM)-based embedders due to their strong instruction-following capabilities. Despite this progress, existing approaches primarily focus on language and image modalities, which also remain the dominant modalities for user-conditioned interactions in current embedders. In this paper, we propose the first Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder (OmniUE), which not only learns a unified embedding space across text, video, and audio by leveraging intermediate-layer representations from dedicated learnable tokens, but also supports omni-interactive querying, enabling users to provide inputs in the form of text, visual regions of interest, and audio spans. Within OmniUE, visual and audio segmenters process diverse user interactions and integrate them with an omni-LLM to produce user-conditioned any-to-any embeddings via context aggregation. To evaluate OmniUE's omni-interactive capabilities, we introduce OmniCHOIR, benchmarking models for omni-interactive compositional audio retrieval based on the given text, video, and audio as well as unimodal or multimodal interaction prompts. OmniUE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines across diverse modalities, with average improvements of 10.5% on textual-interactive video benchmarks (MMEB-v2-video), 1.1% on audio tasks (MAEB), 83.7% on visual-interactive benchmarks (SCaR), and 24.1% on our omni-interactive OmniCHOIR benchmark. We believe that jointly advancing omni-modal representation learning and omni-interactive querying paves the way toward universal embedders.

</details>


### [99] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出电路冷凝方法，通过后训练微调将模型行为压缩到更小的因果图中，在32个设置中30个优于冻结基线，平均缩小8.1倍，最多316倍，并在间接宾语识别中提取出仅含24个头的充分子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法（冻结发现）常返回数百条边，难以人工检查、比较或穷举验证，需要一种能够主动压缩行为相关电路的方法。

Method: 电路冷凝：每轮剪除低归因边，训练低秩适配器仅通过剩余边匹配原模型输出，若任务性能与通用能力保持则保留剪枝，重复迭代。

Result: 在4种行为和8个模型上，冷凝电路在30/32设置中比最强冻结基线更小；无权重更新的搜索在29/32设置中产生更大电路；测试19个电路中11个不可再减；在间接宾语识别中冷凝出24个头（17个有文献记载角色），而冻结基线有61个头（36个无记载）。

Conclusion: 权重更新是电路缩小的关键驱动力，冷凝得到的电路是原始机制的充分子电路而非重构，能跟踪原模型分布并预测错误，且边缘间存在不可独立理解的依赖关系。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [100] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出MM-Spectrum，一种稀疏专家混合框架，通过模态感知路由和异构专家容量解决多光谱数据异质性导致的模态失衡问题，在分子结构解析任务上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态光谱推断分子结构需整合互补但高度异质的信号，直接拼接多光谱序列会因模态失衡和异质性出现性能退化。

Method: 设计显式模态感知路由机制，向路由器暴露光谱身份信息；引入共享专家与交互专家，并配置异构专家容量，以提取模态特有和跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在全模态、双模态和缺失模态场景下，MM-Spectrum均取得一致且显著的改进，消融研究和可解释性分析支持其有效性。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum有效缓解多光谱模态失衡问题，为多模态谱学结构解析提供了鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [101] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层级框架来分析智能体数据生成：将数据表示为因子化对象 (E,q,τ,v)，并通过准确性-复杂度-多样性（ACE）视角将生成视为约束分布设计，揭示现有工作转向执行接地、学习者相对复杂度和超越表面多样性的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据生成研究领域分散、评估异质，常混淆候选构建与验证选择，缺乏统一框架来揭示共同机制和核心挑战。

Method: 建立两层级框架：第一层将智能体数据分解为环境规范、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器，并按主锚点和依赖结构组织生成范式；第二层提出ACE视角，准确度定义可行支持域，复杂度根据学习者能力分配学习密度，多样性控制覆盖与冗余。

Result: 文献分析显示三大转变：生成验证从表面转向执行接地，复杂度校准相对学习者能力，多样性超越表面变化或数据集规模；并讨论了对扩展、数据源、训练模式和自适应学习的影响。

Conclusion: 智能体数据生成的核心挑战不在于生成更多数据，而在于随着智能体和环境演化，持续分配有效、信息丰富且不冗余的经验。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [102] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 基于大型企业近4000名后台员工在2025年8个月内的71万余条提示词及对应大语言模型回复数据，研究了生成式AI使用 sophistication（成熟度/复杂性）的差异及其影响因素。主要发现：高年资员工使用更成熟；不同职能部门间差异显著，战略、数字创新和项目管理部门的成熟度最高；使用成熟度随时间无显著提升，正式AI培训亦无持久改善效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在企业后台办公中的应用日益广泛，但对其使用质量（sophistication）的系统性度量与实证研究匮乏，且管理层缺乏基于证据的干预依据。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提供大规模、细粒度的实证数据与测度方法。

Method: 使用企业内部专有数据，收集2025年八个月内近4000名后台员工（覆盖15个职能部门）的713,564条提示词及大语言模型响应，构建基于提示词特征（如复杂度、上下文利用、多轮交互等）的sophistication指标，并采用描述性统计、跨部门比较、纵向趋势分析及培训效果评估（前后对照）等方法。

Result: 三个核心发现：1）高年资（senior）员工的genAI使用成熟度显著更高，显示领域经验与AI能力互补；2）成熟度在各职能部门间差异大，Strategy、Digital Innovation、Project Management三类职能最高，其共性为关注全公司战略举措与组织变革；3）时间维度上未见成熟度持续改善，且正式AI培训后无持久提升效应。

Conclusion: 研究提供了可复用的sophisticated genAI使用测度工具与实证基线。结果表明成熟使用受领域专长和职能性质驱动，且难以通过短期培训或自然学习快速提升，管理层需定制化支持策略（如嵌入工作流程的针对性子预分析或辅导机制），研究者可据此深挖提升路径。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [103] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于43项常规记录变量、72小时治疗窗口的败血症指数，利用死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号，在跨机构验证中显示出良好的预后分层能力与临床一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有败血症严重程度指数依赖数十年前建立的固定变量和权重，且离散化粗糙、校准队列过时，缺乏直接从患者轨迹学习的替代方案。

Method: 采用回顾性双队列研究（共36,807名Sepsis-3成人患者），开发基于43项常规图表变量的指数，创新性地将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号而非每状态目标，实现非均匀时间步信用分配；在20%永久测试集上通过临床小插图与Spearman相关评估，并用bootstrap获取不确定性区间。

Result: 非幸存者得分比幸存者高1.19-1.64分（0-10量表），在SOFA-2基线各分层内一致；指数变化与乳酸变化相关（rho=0.39），跨机构相关性为同站点相关性的70-77%，外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59（上限0.92和0.90），同时与既有指数相关，零对照接近零。

Conclusion: 该指数提供逐小时预后信息，能有效区分患者结局并符合临床预期，有望作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出关系超图Transformer（RHT），将关系数据库建模为超图，采用五维嵌入和稀疏关系注意力，在Synthea电子病历数据上实现多标签预测，兼顾语义连贯性与计算可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习在医疗等复杂系统中面临数据量大、高维变量、高基数类别、复杂表间依赖和时间序列观测等挑战，现有方法难以统一高效处理。

Method: RHT将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），使用稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度与平均关系度成比例而非实体数量的平方，并提供了开源实现。

Result: 在Synthea数据集上，RHT相比表格、关系和时序图基线产生更语义连贯的嵌入，且计算可扩展；XGBoost在罕见代码召回率上最高，RHT在嵌入语义连贯性上最强，消融实验验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: RHT为关系数据学习提供统一高效框架，在嵌入质量上优于基线，但特定任务指标仍不如传统模型，未来将在MIMIC-IV上临床验证。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [105] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCMAPSS基准，基于NASA C-MAPSS数据集，定义了五个标准化联邦学习任务，用于评估RUL估计中的联邦优化算法，并提供公开代码与数据划分以促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能解决数据稀缺与隐私问题，但当前研究缺乏统一的评估框架，导致结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 基于C-MAPSS构建五个任务，覆盖从IID到极端统计异质性的工业场景，系统评估多种联邦优化算法与神经架构。

Result: 建立了可复现的基线结果，并公开源代码与数据划分，用于标准化联邦预测性维护模型的比较。

Conclusion: 该基准为联邦RUL估计提供了标准基础，推动该领域算法开发与公平对比。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset. We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [106] [Thresholding Post-Quantum Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26792)
*Francesco De Sclavis,Matteo Nardelli,Marco Pedicini*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究后量子数字签名在分布式场景下的门限化问题，按密码学范式（格、哈希、群作用、同源、多变量）分类现有构造工具，并涵盖基于FHE、MPC和ZKP的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门限签名方案主要基于预量子签名，缺乏后量子安全性；NIST对后量子密码的标准化呼吁推动了该方向研究，但缺乏系统性分类与工具梳理。

Method: 作者对后量子签名方案依据底层密码学范式（格问题、单向哈希函数、密码学群作用、同源、多变量系统）进行分门别类，提炼每类中用于构建T-out-of-N门限方案的现有技术组件，同时纳入基于全同态加密、安全多方计算和零知识证明的通用构造思路。

Result: 提出了一套按范式划分的现有构造工具分类体系，覆盖主流后量子签名类型，并指出不同范式下可用于门限化的核心技术。

Conclusion: 为后量子门限签名研究提供了结构化参考框架，有助于识别各范式下的可行构造路径及潜在设计空间。

Abstract: Threshold signature schemes distribute the signing process among $T$ parties out of $N$. They enable a variety of applications and their research is also motivated by a recent NIST call. However, applications are dominated by pre-quantum signatures, which are more efficient but not secure in the post-quantum setting. This paper investigates existing post-quantum signatures, based on a variety of paradigms: lattice problems, one-way (hash) functions, cryptographic group actions, isogenies and multivariate systems. We propose a classification (divided by paradigm) of existing tools that are used to build $T$-out-of-$N$ schemes from digital signatures. We also include general approaches based on FHE, MPC or ZKP.

</details>


### [107] [Information Flow Control in Off-Chain Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26858)
*Stian Lybech,Eun-Young Kang,Riccardo Tonello,Anders Dalskov*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文为含链下组件的区块链智能合约语言建立形式化模型，并利用静态信息流控制技术研究链上与链下组件间的数据完整性与保密性，发现即便无循环结构，链下组件的线程特性（如递归调用编码阻塞）仍会导致信息流控制失效，最后探讨了补救方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链下组件为智能合约提供灵活性（如访问外部数据、跨链互通），但也引入新漏洞；现有模型缺乏对链下组件的抽象，无法系统分析其安全性质，尤其是数据机密性与完整性。

Method: 构建含链下组件的智能合约语言的形式化模型，将链下组件建模为独立线程，采用静态信息流控制技术（类型系统或依赖分析）验证链上与链下之间数据流的保密性和完整性约束。

Result: 信息流控制机制被证明不健全：即使禁止循环结构，链下组件作为独立线程仍可通过递归方法调用编码阻塞操作，从而破坏信息流属性，导致保密性或完整性保证失败。

Conclusion: 结论指出，简单的静态信息流控制不足以应对链下组件的并发特性，需考虑更细粒度的并发原语、终止性分析或新颖的访问控制策略来恢复安全保证。

Abstract: This paper develops a model of a smart-contract language for a blockchain architecture with off-chain components. Off-chain components are pieces of smart contracts that execute at designated locations outside of the network of blockchain nodes, but remain synchronised with the on-chain contract state. They react to changes to the on-chain state, but may also notify the on-chain component about events in the world, e.g. stock prices, weather data etc., or even act as a bridge between different blockchains. This affords greater flexibility for the developer, but may also enable new vulnerabilities. As a concrete example, we use the model to study the problem of ensuring integrity and secrecy of data between the on-chain and off-chain components, using static information flow control techniques. This fails, even in the absence of a loop construct, because off-chain components act as separate threads and can encode a blocking construct e.g. through recursive method calls. We end the paper with a discussion of possible ways to remedy this situation.

</details>


### [108] [Can You Say This for Me? Speaking Up by Proxy in Co-Located Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26185)
*Yue Shen,Rehema Abulikemu,Ryan P. McMahan,Yan Chen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: SecondVoice是一个混合现实系统，通过具身虚拟代理让用户在集体讨论中匿名发声，将观点与发言者分离，以降低社会风险并促进平等参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在面对面协作讨论中，人们常因担心负面人际或职业后果而保留观点，尤其是当需要亲自开口时。现有匿名文本渠道缺乏口语讨论的互动性，无法有效融入实时对话。

Method: 系统通过私有覆盖层让用户以结构化方式指定意图，而非完整表述，随后自动重述并经由虚拟代理将观点加入口语讨论。研究还定义了社会风险下的参与渠道设计空间，并进行了与匿名文本板的对比实验。

Result: 在16名参与者的初步研究中，50%的参与者使用SecondVoice表达了未直接说出的观点，而文本板仅为18.8%。代理传达的观点成功进入口语讨论并引发多轮小组互动，而文本板帖子未观察到此类现象。

Conclusion: SecondVoice在情境中有价值，但存在时机、所有权和重述信任等方面的权衡，表明该设计空间值得进一步探索以优化混合现实中的参与机制。

Abstract: Equal participation in co-located discussion is important for effective collaboration, yet people often hold back when they anticipate negative interpersonal or professional consequences, especially when raising a point requires voicing it themselves. We present SecondVoice, a mixed-reality system that enables people to speak up through an embodied virtual proxy. By separating what is said from who says it, SecondVoice brings hesitant points into the live spoken discussion without putting the speaker on the spot. Using a private overlay, users specify their intent through a structured specification process rather than composing a full utterance. The system reformulates the input and voices it into the conversation through the proxy. We characterize a design space of participation channels under social risk. In a preliminary within-subject study (N = 16), we compare the complete SecondVoice system with an anonymous text-board channel across two group discussion tasks. Half of participants reported using SecondVoice for a point they did not say aloud, compared with 18.8% for the text board. Proxy-delivered points entered the spoken floor and were followed by multi-turn group engagement, which we did not observe after text-board posts. Participants described the channel as situationally valuable but identified tradeoffs around timing, ownership, and trust in reformulation.

</details>


### [109] [Metamorphism: A mathematical challenge for antivirus technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27007)
*Luis M. Augusto*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出使用van Wijngaarden文法实现病毒变形，使变形病毒难以被传统杀毒软件检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变形病毒能生成功能相同但代码高度不同的副本，现有杀毒软件难以检测，需要新的形式化方法来应对。

Method: 利用van Wijngaarden文法的强大生成能力来模拟病毒的变形过程，从而生成无限多样化的病毒变体。

Result: 证明变形机制完全可通过van Wijngaarden文法实现，关键在于杀毒软件需嵌入判定该文法的图灵机。

Conclusion: 变形病毒可通过高级文法形式化实现，检测挑战转化为对相应语言判定问题的计算复杂性。

Abstract: Metamorphic viruses, currently the most advanced computer viruses in the wild, have the unique ability of mutating their own code virtually into infinitely many highly dissimilar copies of themselves that nevertheless have the same functionality. This ability -- metamorphism -- together with other advanced ob- fuscation techniques makes these computer viruses virtually undetectable by the antivirus software available in the market today. In this paper, we show that viral metamorphism is fully attainable by the employment of van Wijngaarden grammars. The challenge then for an antivirus software is to embed the Turing machine that decides the language generated by the grammar.

</details>


### [110] [Invocation-Level Reliability of Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26189)
*Afiya Noorain,Subhranshu Mohanty,Amritesh Banerjee,Abhijit Dasgupta*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 工具使用智能体常因选错工具或生成错误参数而失败，且早期错误会污染后续结果。研究发现，到深度6时，模型因自身早期错误损失约70%的干净上下文能力。核心发现是，在固定金标准轨迹的精确匹配评分下，传播模型的严重性和恢复参数在结构上不可估计，评分规则本身固定了这些参数。作者提出条件状态评分作为补救，可零成本恢复参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用智能体评估中，错误传播的严重性和恢复能力难以测量，且早期错误会静默破坏下游任务。作者希望分离工具选择与参数生成两类错误，并测量模型在干净上下文与自生成上下文下的正确调用率，同时揭示评分规则对参数估计的根本限制。

Method: 在无污染的多步任务（深度1-8）上，对五个开源模型测量干净教师强制上下文和自由运行上下文下的正确调用率。通过精确匹配评分固定金标准轨迹，分析传播模型的严重性和恢复参数的可估计性，提出条件状态评分并应用于缓存的完成结果。

Result: 深度6时约70%的干净上下文能力因自身错误丢失（L6=0.686, 0.684）。在869个中毒步骤中0个正确，580个中毒步骤中0个恢复（期望随机0.0058）。拟合参数返回0.92和0.73，但真实值为1.000，揭示评分规则导致参数固定。条件状态评分将严重性估计从边界移至内部（+0.149, +0.316）。

Conclusion: 固定金标准轨迹的精确匹配评分使错误传播参数不可估计，严重性被迫取边界值，恢复性结构上不可观察。条件状态评分可解除这一限制，零成本恢复可靠的参数估计，为工具使用智能体评估提供更准确的方法。

Abstract: Tool-using agents fail two ways: choosing the wrong tool, or forming wrong arguments, and an early failure of either kind can silently corrupt everything downstream. We measure a correct-invocation rate that separates the two, under both a clean teacher-forced context and the model's own free-running context, on five open-weight models over contamination-free multi-step tasks (depths 1-8). By depth 6, roughly 70% of a model's own clean-context capability is lost to its own earlier mistakes (L6 = 0.686, 0.684). Our central finding concerns the measurement itself. Under exact-match scoring against a fixed gold trajectory, a propagation model's severity and recovery parameters are not merely hard to estimate - they are fixed by the scoring rule. Severity is forced to its boundary (0 of 869 poisoned steps correct); recovery is structurally unobservable (0 of 580 poisoned steps returned on-track, against an expected 0.0058 by chance). Both follow from one mechanism: post-divergence, the gold value is generated by tool constants the model never sees, so it is information the model cannot derive. A fit run anyway returns 0.92 and 0.73 for a quantity that is exactly 1.000 - confident numbers for a parameter the scoring rule already determined. We give the mechanism and a remedy, conditional-on-state scoring, applied retrospectively to cached completions at zero additional cost, which un-pins severity to interior estimates excluding zero (+0.149, +0.316).

</details>


### [111] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出将“可量化性”（能否得到可用药效估计）作为高通量筛选中的独立分诊目标，并证明其可从前序低成本筛选数据中预测，进而优化剂量反应验证的资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有筛选策略仅关注生物活性确认，但确认活性不一定能产生可报告的剂量反应估计，导致昂贵验证实验浪费，缺乏针对可量化性的预测框架。

Method: 构建了可量化性建模框架，将其与生物活性区分开，利用前序筛选特征（而非分子结构）进行预测，并评估跨化学骨架和机制家族的外部泛化能力。

Result: 可量化性可被强预测，主要信息来自筛选响应特征；模型在未知化学骨架和不同检测机制中保持稳健，且成功概率与响应幅度及检测背景显著相关。

Conclusion: 实验可测量性是可预测且独立于生物活性的属性，基于可量化性的分诊能有效改善昂贵剂量反应分析资源的分配。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [112] [Structured Evidence Routing for Incident Risk Prediction from Multimodal Longitudinal EHRs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26191)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出结构化证据路由（router-predictor-reviewer工作流），在纵向电子健康记录上实现与监督基线相当的1年发病风险预测性能，同时提供患者级证据轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向EHR中的风险信号多模态、孤立且分散于不规则病史，现有模型难以兼顾预测性能与可解释性。

Method: 设计三阶段路由-预测-审查框架：路由器将完整索引前EHR压缩为摘要与靶向证据切片；预测器生成证据关联的风险评估；审查者进行批判性修正。对比实验中将路由证据与监督分类器结合，并通过消融验证各组件贡献。

Result: 在5个1年发病任务中达到EHRSHOT监督基线AUROC范围，AUPRC保持竞争力；消融显示路由、实验室证据、任务引导和审查均有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化证据路由可在不牺牲预测性能的前提下提供可追溯的个体化证据，为高 stakes 临床决策支持提供可行范式。

Abstract: Incident risk prediction from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) is challenging because relevant signals are multimodal, weak in isolation, and distributed across irregular patient histories. We propose structured evidence routing, a router-predictor-reviewer workflow that separates full-record access from disease-specific assessment. The router organizes the complete pre-index EHR into a compact summary and targeted evidence slices; the predictor uses this evidence to form an evidence-linked risk assessment, which the reviewer critiques. For comparison with supervised EHRSHOT baselines, we pair the routed evidence summaries with a supervised classifier readout. Across five 1-year incident diagnosis tasks, our method reaches the AUROC range of established supervised EHRSHOT baselines and remains competitive on AUPRC, while exposing a patient-specific evidence trail. Internal pre-readout ablations further suggest that routing, laboratory evidence, task guidance, and review each contribute to performance.

</details>


### [113] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了传统机器学习、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习方法在风能和波浪能场景下的预测性能，发现随机树集成在结构化WEC数据上最优，RF-BiLSTM混合模型在含时空动态的SCADA数据上精度最高，且不存在普适最优架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对多种AI架构在互补可再生能源场景下系统性的横向对比，尤其是针对结构化布局数据与时序监测数据的不同特性，难以指导模型选择。

Method: 在三个数据集（大规模WEC、16机WEC、Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA）上，对八类模型进行统一基准测试，包括Extra Trees、STGCN、RF-BiLSTM等，并基于MAE和R等指标评估。

Result: Extra Trees在WEC数据上相对MLP降低MAE约63.7%；STGCN在SCADA上MAE约167.0 kW、R=0.93；RF-BiLSTM取得最佳MAE=150.5 kW，比LSTM降低约75%，比STGCN提升约10%。

Conclusion: 不同数据特性需要匹配不同模型：随机/提升树适用于结构化代理建模，图网络适合显式空间交互，集成循环混合模型则在非线性表格关系与时间动态共存时表现最佳，不存在通用最优架构。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


### [114] [6.5% of the Neuro-Symbolic Literature Can Be Reproduced from Its Published Artifacts, a Six-Stage Audit Framework and First Instantiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26236)
*Brandon Colelough,Vladimir Martirosyan,Ishan Tamrakar,William Regli,Aditya Kumar,Anh N. Nhu,Dhruv Dubey,Raj Ambavane,Haowei Deng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个六阶段可复现性审计框架，并在神经符号AI子领域实施，发现仅6.52%的研究可复现，大量论文即使声明代码可用仍缺乏完整工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学领域普遍存在可复现性危机，但缺乏系统化、跨文献的审计方法，尤其在神经符号AI这类交叉子领域。

Method: 设计六阶段审计框架：检索去重、标题摘要筛选、全文筛选、代码工件核查、工件清单建立并有限重跑，最终量化可复现率与阻塞原因。

Result: 5497条记录经筛后1304篇合格，仅455篇有可验证代码，成功复现85篇；失败主因是缺失非代码工件（321次）和代码仓库不可用（42次）。

Conclusion: 当前可复现性赤字严重，即使标注代码可用也不可靠；建议强制要求投稿时提供完整、版本化、永久归档的工件包。

Abstract: We present a six-stage framework for auditing the reproducibility of scientific claims across a research literature within the computer science domain, and instantiate our framework for the neuro-symbolic AI (NSAI) subdomain. Instantiating the framework on the NSAI subdomain produced a multi-year audit. Stage one retrieved 5,497 records and removed 3,018 duplicates. Stage two screened the 2,479 unique records at title and abstract, identifying 1,365 self-identified NSAI records, then removed a further 61 at full text for off-topic, non-research, no-quantitative-evaluation, or inaccessible-full-text reasons. Stage three sought a verifiable public code artifact for each of the 1,304 eligible records and found none for 849, leaving 455 to enter the artifact inventory and bounded rerun of stages four and five. We fully or partially reproduced 85 studies, 6.52% of the eligible corpus and 18.68% of attempted reruns. We found that 321 attempted reruns were blocked by missing non- code artifacts and 42 by missing or unusable code repositories. These figures quantify a persistent reproducibility deficit that survives even nominal "code available" declarations, and signal the need for enforced, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles in future NSAI publications. We argue that empirical NSAI papers should be required at submission time to provide complete, versioned, and permanently archived artifact bundles.

</details>


### [115] [Approved Too Late: Verdict Staleness in LLM-Guarded Self-Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26306)
*Ilai Shraga,Roei Eshel,Lior Gorelik*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应性系统中LLM护栏审批的时效性问题，提出Freshness-Bounded Shield方法，将审批过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%，并定义新鲜度契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM护栏在检查时正确的审批可能在执行时已失效，产生TOCTOU风险，现有研究缺乏对审批时效性的系统量化与缓解。

Method: 提出三个度量指标（固定动作重放下的全候选审批变化、Oracle标签的审批过期率、法官条件的使用时失效性），并设计Freshness-Bounded Shield，基于安全边际和特征波动性估计审批有效期，无需显式动力学模型。

Result: 在五个可复现环境中，全候选审批变化率为5.3-48.4%；FBS将Oracle标签过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%；四个LLM法官均存在非零的法官条件使用时失效性。

Conclusion: 审批时效性是LLM护栏的关键风险，FBS能有效缓解；所有审批必须同时满足检查时正确与使用时有效，才能构成完整的新鲜度契约。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) guardrail for a self-adaptive system (SAS) may issue an approval that is correct at check time but stale by actuation. This creates an Execute-stage time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) hazard. We study verdict freshness: whether a guardrail verdict remains valid when used. We distinguish three quantities that answer different questions: all-candidate verdict change under fixed-action replay, oracle-labeled approval expiry on recorded closed-loop trajectories, and judge-conditioned use-time invalidity. Across five reproducible SAS environments, all-candidate verdict-change rates span 5.3-48.4% at a common replay shift of eight simulator steps. We introduce the Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS), which estimates each approval's validity horizon from its safe-side margin and recent feature volatility, without an explicit plant-dynamics model. Using fixed settings documented in the artifact, FBS reduces oracle-labeled approval-expiry rates from 3.4-24.7% to 0-1.8% at the same shift. A separate audit of four LLM judges finds nonzero judge-conditioned use-time invalidity in every approval stream. We formulate a freshness contract: every approval must be correct at check time and remain valid at use time.

</details>


### [116] [ProofEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolution for Formal Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26334)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Eric Xie,Bohan Liu,Shivani Modi,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ProofEvolve，一种神经符号框架，通过进化显式且经过形式验证的符号证明结构（AND-OR证明DAG），结合神经模型提议变异算子与Lean内核验证，实现递归自改进的定理证明，在三项竞赛级基准上取得最高平均求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经证明器未充分利用递归自改进结构：要么通过昂贵的权重更新将证明经验嵌入参数，要么仅保留当前问题内的中间推导；且严重依赖稀疏的整条证明反馈，忽略了失败部分尝试中有用的发现。

Method: ProofEvolve框架中，神经模型提议变异算子（分解、修复、模式重组），符号Lean内核验证每一步转换；在进化循环中计算证明DAG的已验证闭包；问题内，在行为索引归档中进化部分AND-OR证明DAG；跨问题，内核检查的模式提取将新证明的子DAG加入持久模式库；通过类型化模式重组继承已解结果，并将剩余前提作为新子目标暴露。

Result: 在三个竞赛级Lean基准上，ProofEvolve的平均求解率在评估的证明系统中最高。

Conclusion: 进化过程保留了不完整尝试中的已验证结果，并使其可用于后续证明而不削弱形式健全性，从而有效扩展知识边界，实现递归自改进。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving offers a natural foundation for recursive self-improvement in scientific discovery.
