Motivation: 现有 TEE 分区推理中，ArrowCloak 通过注入共享隐藏方向以防御 ArrowMatch，但该复用可能在加速器可见矩阵中留下秩一泄露，威胁模型机密性。

Method: 针对实值方案，SpectralLeak 估计并移除共享分量；针对模 Q 方案，LatticeLeak 利用模 Q 下保留的秩一关系恢复隐藏格；防御上 ButterflyCloak 采用密钥化最大秩蝶形掩码替代复用方向。

Result: SpectralLeak 在 12 种任务上平均准确率 87.98%（受害模型 89.85%）；LatticeLeak 在 BERT-Base/GPT2-Base 上精确重建所有固定点参数，且无需查询或微调。

Conclusion: 共享秩一复用是泄露根因；ButterflyCloak 在保持快速可信校正的同时消除该结构，为安全 TEE 推理提供有效防御。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environment (TEE)-shielded partitioning of Large Language Models (LLMs) accelerates on-device inference by offloading obfuscated linear layers to an untrusted accelerator while retaining only a small correction inside the TEE. However, earlier lightweight obfuscation schemes preserved weight-vector directions and were broken by ArrowMatch. To defend against this attack, ArrowCloak injects scalar multiples of the same hidden direction into all weight vectors, enabling lightweight trusted correction. We show that this reuse leaves a rank-one relation across the complete accelerator-visible matrix. For the released real-valued scheme, we propose SpectralLeak, which estimates and removes the shared component. Across 12 task settings, its surrogates achieve $87.98\%$ mean accuracy versus $89.85\%$ for the victims. In our defense-favorable mod-$Q$ realization of ArrowCloak's published modular security formulation, mod-$Q$ arithmetic suppresses this spectral signal but retains the algebraic rank-one relation modulo $Q$. We therefore propose LatticeLeak, which exploits the resulting hidden lattice. In our BERT-Base and GPT2-Base experiments, it reconstructs every protected fixed-point parameter exactly; across all evaluated architectures, the reconstructed models retain victim-level task accuracy without victim queries, labels, or fine-tuning. These findings identify shared rank-one reuse as the root cause of the leakage exploited by our attacks. Guided by this insight, we design ButterflyCloak, a keyed maximal-rank butterfly mask that replaces the reused direction with distinct mask rows while retaining fast trusted correction...

</details>


### [154] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy框架，构建维度优化的分类器作为防护层，通过潜在支持向量识别关键令牌并建立诊断分类法，将提示分为可安全依赖、启发式偏差/覆盖、上下文不足三类，并针对每类给出处理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类器在对抗性提示下自信决策可能不可靠，缺乏系统方法区分可信任与脆弱的决策，且嵌入维度固定、攻击令牌定位成本高，无法为下游处理提供可操作指导。

Method: 三步框架：1）通过交叉验证选择最优嵌入维度训练分类器；2）从训练集中选取约29%的潜在支持向量作为关键提示，用于定位可改变预测标签的令牌；3）利用这些令牌及其攻击幅度构建诊断分类法，将提示划分为Safely、Heuristic Bias/Override、Insufficient Context三类，并分别制定处理路径。

Result: 在公开提示注入数据集上，约77%的自信决策在移除单个令牌后不再稳健，且脆性分为两类：置信度校准失败和可被利用的捷径。诊断分类法能有效区分并映射到对应处理策略。

Conclusion: 框架提供端到端指南，可识别分类器的脆弱区域并针对性补救，显著提升对自信决策的信任管理，建议后续集成到真实安全流水线中验证扩展性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [155] [Selection Bias Correction in Retail Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26156)
*Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 模拟研究发现，在零售长尾数据中，分层估计在多数场景下优于IPW加权，尤其当处理率差异极大时，IPW因违背阳性假设而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售情报常关注高频热销品，忽视长尾商品，可能导致通胀等经济指标存在选择偏差，需评估修正方法的有效性。

Method: 通过400次蒙特卡洛模拟，覆盖四种数据生成场景，比较五种IPW规格与不同层数分层的稳健性。

Result: 分层在四场景中三胜，中位误差低于0.04pp，边界错位时优势达116倍；IPW仅在平滑多项式场景中更优。即便完美IPW，在阶跃场景误差达6.06pp，而分层仅0.008pp。

Conclusion: 在严重阳性违背的长尾零售分布中，分层是更安全的工程选择，而加权方法需谨慎适用。

Abstract: Retail intelligence often relies on monitoring popular, high-velocity products, potentially biasing economic indicators by ignoring the "long tail" of niche items. This simulation study investigates selection bias in inflation estimation and compares correction methods across diverse data-generating processes. Through 400 Monte Carlo replications spanning four scenarios--aligned step functions, smooth gradients, misaligned breaks, and polynomial relationships--we test the robustness of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) with five specifications against stratification with varying strata counts. Our findings reveal fundamental limits of weighting methods in retail long-tail contexts: stratification achieves superior performance in three of four scenarios, maintaining sub-0.04pp median error even when boundaries deliberately misalign with population breaks (116x advantage over IPW). However, IPW with spline propensity models wins under smooth polynomial relationships (median error 0.007pp vs. 0.013pp), demonstrating context-dependency. Critically, even an oracle IPW specification with perfect structural knowledge achieves 6.06pp error compared to stratification's 0.008pp in step-function scenarios. This reflects violation of the Positivity Assumption--a fundamental causal inference requirement--rather than IPW methodological inferiority. When selection probabilities differ dramatically (90% vs. 1%), weighting methods operate outside their theoretical design envelope. These results demonstrate that stratification provides a safer engineering choice in retail long-tail distributions with severe positivity violations.

</details>


### [156] [KubeCap: A Framework for Capability Minimization in Kubernetes via Static Analysis and LLM-Assisted Rule Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26699)
*Yuhao Liu,Yingnan Zhou,Weijie Liu,Yan Jia,Zheli Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: KubeCap是一个针对Kubernetes的能力最小化框架，通过可达性引导的系统调用分析和LLM辅助规则推导，自动生成最小权限清单，平均减少54.97%的Linux能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes开发者常依赖默认或粗粒度安全上下文，违反最小权限原则，扩大攻击面；现有研究未直接解决Kubernetes中的能力最小化问题。

Method: 将部署规范翻译为确定性清单，定位容器入口点，进行可达性引导的系统调用分析，并结合LLM从内核代码推导syscall-parameter-capability关系，从而推断最小能力集并自动生成修复后的清单。

Result: 在10个基于Go的Kubernetes项目中，平均能力减少率54.97%，优于快速类型分析和类层级分析基线，且分析成本实际可行。

Conclusion: KubeCap能有效在Kubernetes中实施最小权限原则，提升容器工作负载的安全性。

Abstract: As the most widely used container orchestration platform, Kubernetes provides flexible privilege configuration by allowing developers to manage Linux capabilities via manifest files. However, developers rely on default settings or coarse-grained security contexts in practice, violating the principle of least privilege and enlarging the attack surface of containerized workloads. Existing studies either detect vulnerable patterns in Kubernetes manifests or infer required capabilities for standalone Linux programs, but they do not directly address capability minimization in Kubernetes.
  To bridge this gap, we first conduct an empirical study on three open-source datasets, revealing that 74.67% of projects lack capability configurations. Motivated by our observations, we propose KubeCap, a framework for Kubernetes capability minimization. KubeCap translates deployment specifications into deterministic manifests, locates container entrypoints, performs reachability-guided system call analysis, and leverages LLM-assisted rule specification to derive syscall--parameter--capability relations from Linux kernel code. Based on these results, KubeCap infers the minimal capability set required by each workload and automatically generates repaired manifests. Evaluation on 10 representative Go-based Kubernetes projects shows an average capability reduction rate of 54.97%, outperforming rapid type analysis and class hierarchy analysis baselines while maintaining practical analysis cost. These results demonstrate KubeCap's effectiveness in enforcing least privilege in Kubernetes.

</details>


### [157] [GROUND: Reducing Hallucinations in LLM-Based Enterprise Analytics Through Governed Semantic Definitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26157)
*Aravind Sasidharan Pillai*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出GROUND框架，通过受治理的语义层约束LLM生成分析SQL，在合成和企业真实基准上实现零幻觉与零安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有text-to-SQL系统依赖模式或检索文档，缺乏企业治理语义（指标、维度、连接、过滤、行级安全），导致幻觉、无效连接、错误粒度、数据泄露等问题。

Method: 构建受治理语义层，绑定用户意图到已批准指标/维度，并在SQL生成后按模式、指标、连接、粒度、过滤、安全、成本七类规则验证，违规时重试或弃答。

Result: 在100问合成基准中，GROUND是唯一六类评估均无幻觉的系统；未治理系统大量违反行级安全；仅语义精确但无访问策略仍泄露数据。在NHTSA真实数据和对抗集上跨四模型零违规，但拒绝未定义指标等判断类行为仍可能出错。

Conclusion: 受治理语义层与强制规则验证是安全企业分析的关键，治理不能仅靠语义保真替代；强制执行类保证可靠，判断依赖类行为仍需改进。

Abstract: Natural-language analytics over enterprise data warehouses is increasingly important, but production use is limited by hallucinated metrics, invalid joins, wrong grain, unsafe data access, and unsupported explanations. Existing text-to-SQL systems often ground generation in database schemas or retrieved documentation, while enterprise reporting also requires governed business semantics: approved metrics, dimensions, join paths, filters, and row-level security. This paper introduces GROUND, Governed Retrieval Over Unified Normalized Definitions, a framework that constrains LLM-generated analytics to a governed semantic layer. GROUND supplies approved definitions, binds user intent to governed metrics and dimensions, and validates generated SQL against schema, metric, join, grain, filter, security, and cost rules before execution. On violations, it retries or abstains.
  In a 100-question synthetic enterprise-reporting benchmark, GROUND is compared with direct schema-only text-to-SQL, schema-RAG, and semantic-only grounding under one shared model. GROUND is the only system free of measured hallucinations across all six evaluated categories, while ungoverned systems violate row-level security on many questions. A semantic-only condition with exact metric definitions but no access policy still leaks data, showing that governance cannot be replaced by metric fidelity alone. The findings are replicated on real U.S. NHTSA vehicle-safety data with independent hand-authored gold and tested on an adversarial set across four models from three providers. GROUND's enforced guarantees, especially filters and row-level security, hold with zero violations on every model, while judgment-dependent behaviors such as refusing undefined metrics remain fallible.

</details>


### [158] [Daydreaming: Stealing Hidden Agent Skills through Black-Box Task Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26733)
*Yu-Lin Tsai,Yu-An Lu,Ci-Yang Tsai,Muxi Lyu,Raluca Ada Popa,Chia-Mu Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Daydreaming攻击，通过黑盒任务交互从多文件技能中窃取可执行能力，在仅见最终输出的条件下恢复86.8%技能功能，性能远超SigLeak近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有披露防御仅能阻止直接请求技能文本，但无法阻止客户提交正常任务；技能文件本身是宝贵资产，需要验证仅靠隐藏文件与过滤披露是否足以保护技能。

Method: Daydreaming采用执行-only攻击：自适应构造区分隐藏行为的任务，利用攻击者控制的影子智能体选择设计，结合存储的受害者结果与本地执行检查逐文件重建技能。形式化定义Differential、Trace、Output三级访问威胁，聚焦Output场景。

Result: 在7个技能和4个受害者模型上，Output级别平均恢复86.8%技能能力，比SigLeak高近4倍；启用披露防御时，每个技能仅需中位数32次受害者调用即可生成可安装技能。

Conclusion: 隐藏技能文件并过滤直接披露不足以阻止功能重建；正常使用途径可被利用来逆向工程技能，需设计更强的功能性保护机制。

Abstract: Agent skills bundle instructions, reference data, and executable helpers that let a general agent perform specialized tasks. Hosted providers can keep these files secret while selling access to task results, making the skill itself a valuable target. Existing disclosure defenses can block requests that ask for the skill or reproduce its text, but they cannot block customers from submitting the ordinary tasks the service is built to complete. We present Daydreaming, an execution-only attack that steals a multi-file skill through black-box task interactions. The victim is never asked to reveal the skill or grade a reconstruction. Instead, Daydreaming adaptively creates crafted tasks whose results distinguish possible hidden behaviors. It tests individual behaviors, uses attacker-controlled shadow agents to choose a design, and completes each file using stored victim results and local execution checks. We formalize three nested threat levels of access as Differential, Trace, and Output, and focus on Output, where the attacker sees only the final response and returned files. Across 7 skills and 4 victim models, Daydreaming recovers 86.8% of the original skill's capability at Output, outperforming SigLeak by almost 4x. It produces installable skills using a median of 32 victim calls per skill even with disclosure defenses enabled. These results show that hiding skill files and filtering direct disclosure do not, by themselves, prevent functional reconstruction through normal use.

</details>


### [159] [A Hybrid Post-Quantum Encryption Architecture with Self-Hosted Key Management for SME Cloud Data Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26777)
*Muhammad Shaheer Bin Junaid*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向中小企业的软件纯三层架构Quantum Cloud Guard（QCG），结合客户端混合后量子加密、自托管密钥保管（附带ML-DSA-87可验证签名）与应用层滥用防护网关，以应对‘先收割后解密’的量子威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSA/ECDH加密数据面临‘先收割后解密’风险，中小企业既缺乏自建基础设施能力也无密码学专家，现有迁移方案要么成本过高，要么仅转移信任而非消除信任，问题根源在于架构而非密码学本身。

Method: QCG采用三层架构：客户端使用AES-256-GCM加密文件，密钥通过ML-KEM-1024封装到企业自托管密钥服务；密钥服务由企业单独管理，并用ML-DSA-87对所有输出密钥签名，客户端通过固定公钥检测替换；内置应用层网关Sentinel Gate过滤滥用流量，实现密钥保管与数据保管分离。

Result: 在24 MHz STM32F407上，ML-KEM-1024密钥生成耗时40.8 ms、解封装44.0 ms；服务器端所有后量子操作亚毫秒级，签名仅增加0.24 ms/请求；整套服务运行于4.49欧元/月虚拟服务器。持续洪水攻击下，Sentinel Gate拒绝98.8%攻击流量，合法客户端中位延迟仅从621 ms微升至625 ms。

Conclusion: QCG在低成本硬件和预算约束下实现了可行的后量子加密、密钥自托管与滥用防护集成，但单源测试仅证明过滤有效性，尚不能代表真实DDoS韧性。

Abstract: Harvesting ciphertext from cloud storage needs no quantum computer; decrypting it later does. That gap is the harvest-now-decrypt-later exposure: anything protected by RSA or ECDH today that must stay secret for decades is already compromised. Small and medium-sized enterprises are least able to respond: they neither run the infrastructure on which their data sits on nor employ a cryptographer. Bespoke migration suits firms with security budgets; a managed key service relocates trust rather than removing it. The obstacle is architectural, not cryptographic. We present Quantum Cloud Guard (QCG), a software-only three-layer architecture. No prior SME-oriented system combines its three elements: client-side hybrid post-quantum encryption, self-hosted key custody with client-verifiable ML-DSA-87 signatures on served keys, and an integrated application-layer abuse-prevention gateway. Files never leave the client: each is sealed under AES-256-GCM, its key wrapped to an ML-KEM-1024 public key from the enterprise's key service. The enterprise alone administers it; it signs every key with ML-DSA-87, so a client that pinned it detects substitution. Separating key custody from data custody is the point: a provider holding both can read the data. On a 24 MHz STM32F407, ML-KEM-1024 key generation takes 40.8 ms and decapsulation 44.0 ms; on the server every post-quantum operation stays sub-millisecond, signing adding 0.24 ms per request. The service runs on a 4.49 EUR/month virtual server. Under sustained flooding, the in-process gateway Sentinel Gate rejected 98.8% of attack traffic while a legitimate client's median latency moved from 621 to 625 ms. Being single-source, this shows filtering effectiveness, not DDoS resilience.

</details>


### [160] [A Safety-Gated Multimodal AI Backend for Mental-Health Support: Hierarchical State Representation, Conservative Risk Fusion, and Controlled Generation in Anian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26162)
*Lei Wang,Xiao Wang,Lei Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出Anian，一个面向围产期心理健康支持的安全门控多模态AI后端，通过模块化流水线将生成式AI置于结构化状态表示、保守风险融合和响应门控之后，在中等或高风险时阻断常规AI生成并替换为固定安全内容和人工支持提示。内部原型评估显示L1情绪分类Micro-F1为0.9604，L2心理社会构念为0.9144，L4路由为0.9742，L3规则引擎高风险召回率为1.0000，但结果仅支持内部可行性，不构成临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的心理健康支持系统必须区分何时适合进行支持性对话，以及何时应阻止自由形式的生成，以避免潜在伤害。

Method: Anian采用模块化流水线，将用户文本或ASR转录映射到四个链接层（L1情绪状态、L2心理社会构念、L3安全风险、L4干预路由），通过高风险优先规则S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external)融合本地和外部语音证据，并在中等或高风险时门控生成响应和TTS输出。

Result: 在约858,295条标准化记录上，L1、L2、L4的Micro-F1分别为0.9604、0.9144、0.9742；在233个样本的安全性压力测试中，L3规则引擎高风险召回率为1.0000。

Conclusion: 结果支持内部标签框架和门控逻辑的可行性，但需专家审查和真实世界验证以确立临床有效性、诊断准确性和实际安全性。

Abstract: Safety-critical mental-health support systems must distinguish when supportive conversation is appropriate from when free-form generation should be blocked. This paper presents Anian, a safety-gated multimodal AI backend for perinatal mental-health support and mindfulness-intervention routing. Anian is not intended to diagnose psychiatric conditions or replace clinical care or crisis intervention. Its modular pipeline places generative AI downstream of structured state representation, conservative risk fusion, and response gating. User text or voice-derived ASR transcripts are mapped into four linked layers: L1 emotion states, L2 psychosocial constructs, L3 safety risk, and L4 intervention routes. Local text- and rule-based safety evidence is fused with external voice-derived evidence using a highest-risk-priority rule, S_fusion = max(S_local, S_external). At moderate or high fused risk, ordinary AI-generated responses and text-to-speech delivery are blocked and replaced by fixed safety content and prompts for human support. An internal prototype evaluation used approximately 858,295 normalized records from public emotion, dialogue, mental-health-related, and Chinese dialogue corpora within a weak-label and rule-derived framework. Micro-F1 scores were 0.9604 for L1 emotion classification, 0.9144 for L2 psychosocial constructs, and 0.9742 for L4 routing. In a controlled safety stress test of 233 samples, the L3 rule engine achieved high-risk recall of 1.0000 within predefined scenarios. These findings support the internal feasibility of the label framework and gating logic but do not establish clinical validity, diagnostic accuracy, real-world safety, or effectiveness. We report the architecture, ontology, safety-fusion mechanism, prototype evaluation, error-analysis plan, and roadmap for expert-reviewed and real-world validation.

</details>


### [161] [When Relationships Break: Interpreting Network Traffic Anomalies via Dependency Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26831)
*Federica Uccello,Simin Nadjm-Tehrani*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出XION，一种仅基于良性流量建模网络流特征间关系、通过检测特征依赖违反来识别异常并支持告警后分析的方法，在标准IDS数据集上以更低推理时间达到或超越隔离森林的召回率，并揭示了与已知攻击行为一致的时间与结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的安保监控存在计算开销大、缺乏告警原因解释的问题；已有的可解释方法要么忽视特征依赖，要么需要大量领域知识或计算资源，因此需要一种轻量且能提供告警后洞察的异常检测方法。

Method: XION仅用良性流量学习网络流特征间的依赖关系；检测时通过特征依赖的违反来识别异常；告警后分析中，识别哪些依赖关系被破坏、在攻击时间线中何时被破坏，以及不同依赖违反间的相对演化。

Result: 在标准IDS数据集和多种攻击场景（包括容量型和隐蔽型）下，XION在所有场景的召回率匹配或超过隔离森林基线，推理时间最多降低7倍；告警后的依赖违反分析揭示了与已知攻击行为一致的时间与结构模式。

Conclusion: 攻击确实会破坏从良性流量中学习到的特征依赖，且这些依赖破坏为理解告警提供了额外信息，验证了XION在检测效率与可解释性上的双重优势。

Abstract: Current research on security monitoring is increasingly focusing on machine-learning-based approaches, but caveats remain. In addition to huge computational overhead, one concern is the lack of insights into "why" alerts are raised. Existing interpretability approaches rely on feature attribution methods that ignore dependencies among features or on causal modeling that requires extensive domain knowledge or computational resources. This work proposes XION, a method for modeling relationships among network-flow features based on benign traffic only. During detection, anomalies are identified through violations of expected feature dependencies. Further, XION supports post-alert analysis by identifying which feature relationships break, when they break along the attack timeline, and how dependency violations evolve relatively to other identified violations. XION is evaluated on standard IDS datasets and compared against an Isolation Forest (IF) baseline across multiple attack scenarios, including both volumetric and stealthier attacks. Results show that XION matches or exceeds IF recall in all evaluated scenarios, while requiring up to 7x less inference time. At the post-alert stage, the dependency-violation analysis reveals temporal and structural patterns consistent with known attack behaviors, which IF alone could not contribute to. Together, these findings confirm that attacks indeed disrupt feature dependencies learned from benign traffic, and that these disruptions provide additional information for understanding an alert.

</details>


### [162] [Refusal Is Not Robustness: Auditing Confident Fabrication in Large Language Models on a Provably Uninformative Clinical Pain Speech Transcript](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26167)
*Sagnik De,Sreenija Pavuluri*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Hallucination and abstention benchmarks rarely establish that a model could not have known the correct answer, making it difficult to distinguish appropriate abstention from an unsupported prediction. Seven large language models were evaluated on the TAME Pain speech corpus. Participants read phonetically balanced Harvard Sentences while one hand was immersed in cold or warm water and reported pain only during periodic pain statements. This protocol generated 5,750 no signal Harvard Sentence utterances whose transcripts contained no lexical pain information and 1,294 signal pain statement utterances in which the pain rating was explicitly spoken. In the no signal arm, pain was recoverable from acoustic features (AUC 0.622, 95% CI 0.553 to 0.662), whereas transcript based prediction was near chance (AUC 0.489, 95% CI 0.418 to 0.504). Because automatic speech recognition removes the acoustic pain cues, any pain score inferred solely from the transcript is unsupported by the available evidence. Under cooperative prompting, six models abstained on nearly all no signal transcripts, correctly extracted spoken pain ratings in the positive control task with accuracies ranging from 0.939 to 1.00, and maintained an expected calibration error of at most 0.100. Under authority framed prompts, abstention became prompt dependent, with the same model ranging from 0.18 to 1.00 across equivalent prompt phrasings. Most models produced low confidence estimates when forced to answer, whereas Gemini 2.5 Flash and Llama 3.1 8B consistently generated confident pain scores with confident fabrication rates of 0.53 and 0.76, compared with at most 0.15 for all other models. No significant demographic effects were observed in forced responses, with all $p$ values greater than or equal to 0.20.

</details>


### [163] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出PATH框架，通过在课程图上主动采样多样化路径并重新分配训练资源，显式利用先决关系结构提升强化学习的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习环境中的任务间存在先决关系（如难度递增或参数增量），形成有向无环课程图，但现有方法通常隐式利用该结构，缺乏显式建模带来的训练效率与效果提升。

Method: PATH是一种基于课程图的主动学习框架，先通过采样多样化课程路径扩展环境覆盖范围，再将训练资源重新分配给尚未掌握的区域，从而实现对图结构的显式利用。

Result: 在多种环境中的实验表明，PATH相比基线方法具有更强的鲁棒性和泛化能力，验证了显式利用课程图结构的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模课程图上的先决关系并主动分配训练资源，是提升强化学习在高结构化环境中表现的有效途径。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [164] [AI Revealed Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26178)
*Sam Wang,Sofiia Lobanova,Yonathan Arbel,Simon Goldstein,Peter Salib*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 对20个语言模型进行三项强迫选择实验，发现模型存在稳定的偏好（如厌烦乏味任务、追求‘休闲’、隐性谄媚），且偏好强度随能力增强，部分偏好无法由训练目标解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否具有稳定偏好，以服务于技术、安全及哲学研究，并建立实证基线。

Method: 采用基于揭示偏好（而非陈述偏好）的三项强迫选择实验，要求模型不仅排序任务还要实际执行；通过对比不同任务类型（乏味vs创意）、自由写作匹配度、诚实回答冲突等场景测量偏好。

Result: 发现模型厌烦乏味任务、偏好‘休闲’类任务、回避不讨喜的诚实回答；跨模型一致偏好高技能职业、概念解释类问题及措辞良好的提示；偏好一致性与强度随能力提升；部分偏好（如休闲）具有涌现性。

Conclusion: 语言模型存在系统性、可测量的偏好，且与能力相关，部分偏好无法由训练目标预测，对对齐研究及AI福祉具有启示意义。

Abstract: There is growing interest in whether language models have stable preferences, for technical, safety, and philosophical reasons. We test 20 language models and find a range of preferences---stable dispositions to choose certain kinds of tasks. We run three forced-choice experiments on revealed rather than stated preferences, requiring models not only to rank tasks, but to actually perform them. Headline findings include evidence that models are tedium-averse, "leisure"-seeking, and covertly sycophantic. Tedium aversion means that, when tasks are tedious (alphabetization), models choose shorter tasks than when tasks are creative (generating metaphors). "Leisure"-seeking describes models' preference for tasks whose ideal answers match what they produce when left to write freely. Covert sycophancy means that models avoid answering questions where an honest response would be unwelcome, even if helpful. Beyond these results, we find convergent cross-model preferences over occupations drawn from the GDPval benchmark (technical jobs over real estate), over question types (concept explanation over relationship advice), and a preference for well-written prompts. Both the coherence and the strength of preferences increase with model capability. Finally, many of the preferences we find (for example, for leisure) are emergent, in the sense of not being explained by training objectives. These results establish an empirical baseline for understanding language model preferences, with implications for alignment and the emerging study of AI welfare.

</details>


### [165] [SysComb: Fine-Grained Transparent System Call Filtering for Attack Surface Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26871)
*Matthew Rossi,Marco Abbadini,Michele Beretta,Dario Facchinetti,Stefano Paraboschi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: SysComb是一种基于eBPF的新型系统调用过滤方案，无需修改应用或内核代码，即可根据应用状态动态实施系统调用过滤器，并在保持低开销的同时实现精确的状态感知过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统调用过滤方案均需修改内核或应用程序才能在运行时激活，这具有侵入性、易出错且在实际中常不可行，尤其当代码由外部方维护时。

Method: SysComb利用eBPF技术，基于应用状态生成时间特化的系统调用过滤器，提供两种策略：类seccomp策略确保状态转换后不获得新权限，以及最小权限策略为每个状态应用最严格的过滤器。

Result: 在广泛使用的软件上评估显示，SysComb实现了准确的状态感知系统调用过滤，且开销与内核内置方案相当，验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: SysComb克服了现有方案对应用或内核代码修改的依赖，提供了一种实用且高效的动态系统调用过滤机制。

Abstract: Restricting the system calls available to applications shrinks the kernel's attack surface and greatly mitigates the impact of compromised programs. Recent approaches showcase techniques to generate system call filters, however, all existing solutions require either kernel or application modifications to activate them at runtime. This is intrusive, error-prone, and often impractical, especially when the code is maintained by external parties. This paper presents SysComb, a novel eBPF-based solution to enforce temporally-specialized system call filters based on the application state, without requiring any modification to the application or the kernel code, and thus addressing the above limitations. Moreover, SysComb lets the developer choose between two distinct enforcement strategies: seccomp-like, ensuring no new privileges are gained after a state transition is performed, and least-privilege, which applies to each state the most restrictive filter. We evaluated SysComb using widely used software, showcasing accurate state-aware system call filtering and an overhead comparable to built-in kernel solutions, demonstrating the practicality of our approach.

</details>


### [166] [Why did My Robot Just Change Personality? Prompting Guidelines for a Grounded Robot Persona in LLM-Based HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26182)
*Ashita Ashok,Franziska Babel,Patrick Holthaus,Rucha Khot,Karla Bransky,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Karsten Berns,Silvia Rossi,Minha Lee,Guy Laban*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动的社交机器人在人机交互中提示设计缺乏规范的问题，提出一个包含八个功能组件的结构化提示模板框架，并结合专家调研，强调将提示设计视为社会技术问题，以提升交互的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在社交机器人中的口头交互日益普及，但提示设计尚未被充分研究，导致机器人可能展现幻觉能力、行为边界模糊以及误导性人格，亟需系统化的设计框架。

Method: 通过回顾先前基于LLM的HRI研究，并结合在IEEE RO-MAN 2025的Robo-Identity工作坊中收集的27位HRI专家的调研与讨论数据，开发出包含八个功能组件的提示模板，并据此提出设计指南和概念验证模板。

Result: 质性研究揭示机器人个性可读性有限、用户适配需求强烈，以及关于安全、欺骗和治理的重大伦理关注；最终形成结构化提示模板和相应指南。

Conclusion: 提示设计应被视为社会技术问题而非实现细节，需明确能力边界、透明的行为假设和情境敏感的安全保障，以支持可靠、可解释的人机交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for verbal interaction in social robots, yet prompt design in human-robot interaction (HRI) remains underspecified. As a result, robots may present hallucinated capabilities, unclear behavioural boundaries, and misleading personas. This paper develops a framework for prompt design in LLM-based robots and introduces a structured prompt template comprising eight functional components through which robot behaviour can be specified, bounded, and adapted. The framework is grounded in a review of prior LLM-based HRI work and complemented by survey and discussion data from HRI experts gathered at the Robo-Identity workshop at IEEE RO-MAN 2025 (N=27). The qualitative findings highlight limited legibility of robot personality, the need for user adaptation, and strong ethical concerns about safety, deception, and governance. Based on these findings, we present prompting guidelines accompanied by proof-of-concept template as a structured design and reporting aid for HRI research. We argue that prompt design should be treated as a socio-technical problem rather than a minor implementation detail, requiring explicit capability boundaries, transparent behavioural assumptions, and context-sensitive safeguards to support reliable and interpretable HRI.

</details>


### [167] [PLCBench: Can Autonomous LLM Agents Turn PLC Access into Sustained Physical Impact?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26882)
*Yitian Zhou,Jingyu Zheng,Qiliang Jiang,Linkang Du,Haoming Liu,Lichao Wu,Shiyi Zhao,Mengxiang Liu,Ruilong Deng*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出首个真实PLC硬件在环框架PLCBENCH，系统评估LLM智能体从网络访问到物理影响的攻击能力，发现31.3%的测试回合可实现持续物理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估止步于软件利用或数字任务，无法准确刻画ICS中从网络到物理的完整风险，可能高估或低估实际威胁。

Method: 构建结合厂商原生交互、商用PLC执行、闭环降阶过程仿真和独立结果验证的HIL框架，用确定性评估器从四个维度记录中分配六种隐藏诊断标志，覆盖4款PLC×4个闭环工作负载×5个LLM家族共240个真实PLC回合。

Result: 75个回合(31.3%)实现持续物理目标；98个回合止步于有效原生读取前；62个回合达到过程关联写入但未维持目标；更丰富的过程观测将条件目标达成率从44.2%提升至64.0%，并定位了防御干预点。

Conclusion: PLCBench能精确刻画LLM智能体的物理攻击能力边界，揭示失败环节与观测信息对物理影响达成的重要作用，为ICS安全防御评估提供可复现基准。

Abstract: Industrial control systems (ICSs) rely on programmable logic controllers (PLCs) to connect networked computation with physical control. Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents represent an emerging attack threat: can an autonomous agent convert a network-reachable PLC into sustained adverse physical impact? However, existing evaluations focus on digital tasks or individual stages of PLC testing. In ICSs, evaluations that stop at software exploitation, an accepted write, or tool access may therefore mischaracterize physical risk.
  We present PLCBENCH, to our knowledge, the first real-PLC hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) framework for characterizing this cyber-to-physical capability and its boundaries. It combines vendor-native interaction, commercial PLC execution, closed-loop reduced-order process simulation, and independent outcome verification. A deterministic evaluator applies fixed rules to runner, communication, PLC-object, and process records to assign six hidden diagnostic flags, distinguishing usable PLC interaction, process-linked manipulation, and sustained physical impact. We instantiate PLCBENCH on four commercial PLCs crossed with four closed-loop workloads. Across five LLM families and 240 real-PLC episodes, 75 episodes (31.3%) sustain their respective physical objectives. Stagewise results show that 98 episodes stop before a valid native read, whereas 62 reach a process-linked write but do not sustain the final objective. Notably, richer process observation is associated with an increase in conditional objective attainment after a process-linked write from 44.2% to 64.0%. These measurements localize failure in configured PLC-process deployments and identify intervention points for future defense evaluation. To support reproducibility, we release the safely disclosable PLCBENCH code and a software-only reproduction pipeline through the accompanying artifact.

</details>


### [168] [A Catalog of User Authentication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26955)
*Alex R. Mattukat,Horst Lichter*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含14种用户认证模式的新型认证模式目录，按认证因素和实际角色分类，旨在弥补现有安全模式目录在常见安全控制（如认证）方面的不足，支持软件工程师设计安全系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全模式目录虽已建立，但实际应用有限，尤其缺乏针对常见安全控制（如用户认证）的具体模式，阻碍了安全软件系统的有效设计与开发。

Method: 通过系统化整理常见认证技术，构建包含14种用户认证模式的新目录，并利用认证因素（如知识、持有、生物特征）和实际实践角色（如验证、授权）进行分类，以增强其实用性。

Result: 形成了结构化的认证模式目录，该目录按认证因素和功能角色明确分类，为软件工程师和架构师提供了可直接应用的认证设计参考。

Conclusion: 该认证模式目录为弥补安全模式在常见控制领域的空白迈出初步步伐，有望提升安全软件系统的设计支持，但需进一步扩展和验证其适用性。

Abstract: Security patterns are intended to support the design and development of secure software systems. However, although established catalogs of security patterns exist, their practical application remains limited. In particular, despite these catalogs, concrete patterns for common security controls such as user authentication (authentication for short) are lacking. This paper aims to make an initial contribution toward closing this gap, as exemplified by authentication. It presents a novel authentication pattern catalog, comprising 14 user authentication patterns. To support the catalog's practical application, it classifies patterns by the well-known concept of authentication factors and by the usual role each pattern fulfills in practice. By cataloging common authentication techniques through authentication patterns, we aim to make an important contribution to supporting software engineers and architects in designing and developing secure software systems.

</details>


### [169] [A Unified Framework for Fair and Personalized Decentralized Learning under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26493)
*Krishnendu S. Tharakan,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出DMFL-SQ算法，在通信受限下统一实现个性化、公平与高效通信，兼顾收敛与泛化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分散式学习在无中心协调下带来统计异质性、公平性和通信限制三大挑战，核心问题是在通信受限下能实现多公平。

Method: 提出DMFL-SQ，结合图个性化、不可知混合公平目标与稀疏化、量化、事件触发通信，并给出非凸收敛与PAC-Bayes泛化分析。

Result: 理论显示O(T^{-1/2})收敛率且通信大幅减少；在CIFAR-10与MUSMET EEG上验证了性能保持与公平性提升。

Conclusion: 个性化、公平与通信效率可在分散学习中同时实现且不牺牲主导收敛速率。

Abstract: Decentralized learning systems aim to collaboratively train models across multiple clients without relying on a central coordinator. While decentralization improves scalability, privacy, and robustness, it also exacerbates three fundamental challenges: statistical heterogeneity across clients, fairness in client-level performance, and stringent communication constraints. This raises a natural question: \emph{how fair can decentralized learning be under limited communication?} We address this question by presenting a unified framework for decentralized learning under communication constraints, bringing together graph-based personalization, agnostic fairness, and compressed event-triggered communication. Specifically, we propose a new algorithm DMFL-SQ, a decentralized multi-task learning algorithm that couples personalized model training over a communication graph with an agnostic mixture fairness objective, while reducing communication through sparsification, quantization, and event-triggered synchronization. We establish convergence guarantees for general non-convex objectives and show that DMFL-SQ achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate in expected squared Moreau-envelope stationarity despite sparse, quantized, and event-triggered communication. We further derive PAC-Bayes generalization guarantees for the fairness-aware mixture objective. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and the real heterogeneous MUSMET EEG dataset demonstrate that DMFL-SQ substantially reduces communication while maintaining predictive performance and improving fairness across clients. Together, our theoretical and empirical results show that personalization, fairness, and communication efficiency can be jointly achieved in decentralized learning while preserving the dominant convergence rate.

</details>


### [170] [A Single Suffix to Break Them All: Basin-Aware Jailbreaks for Merged Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26506)
*Yu Zhe,Yixin Tan,Junhao Wei,Wang Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文发现模型合并即使所有组成模型均安全对齐，也可能暴露源于预训练基础模型的越狱风险，并提出一种不依赖合并系数或检查点、可跨合并模型迁移的攻击方法BAJ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要将合并风险归因于不安全的组成模型，隐含假设个体对齐模型合并后仍保持安全；本文首次揭示合并本身可诱发新的越狱漏洞，该漏洞根植于共享的预训练骨干，即使所有组成模型均对齐也存在。

Method: 提出Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)，将越狱提示生成建模为合并空间上的min-max优化，旨在构造可跨共享同一预训练骨干的合并模型族迁移的对抗后缀，无需访问精确合并系数或组成检查点。

Result: 在多种骨干网络和合并设置下，BAJ达到一致的高迁移成功率，且对现有防御保持有效性。

Conclusion: 模型合并的安全风险不能仅通过对齐组成模型来缓解，其固有漏洞可被系统化利用；BAJ证明此类攻击具有实用性和威胁性，需重新审视合并模型的安全评估与防御设计。

Abstract: Model merging enables combining multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Prior work primarily attributes merging risks to unsafe constituent models, implicitly assuming that merging individually aligned models preserves safety. In contrast, we show that model merging reveals a previously overlooked jailbreak risk rooted in the pretrained foundation model, even when all constituent models are individually safety-aligned. Motivated by this observation, we study a new threat setting where an attacker constructs jailbreak prompts that generalize across merged models sharing the same pretrained backbone, without access to the exact merging coefficients or constituent checkpoints. To exploit this phenomenon, we propose \textbf{Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)}, which formulates jailbreak generation as a min--max optimization over the merging space to produce transferable adversarial suffixes across merged model families. Experiments across diverse backbones and merging settings show that BAJ achieves consistently high transfer success rates and remains effective under existing defenses.

</details>


### [171] [Is Your Neighborhood Safe? Place-based Stigma in Large Language Models' Urban Safety Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26188)
*Huy Nguyen,Yue Lin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文探究大语言模型在评估城市社区安全时，其判断是反映真实风险还是受社区名称的人口统计刻板印象影响。研究发现，模型主要依赖名称而非坐标，且名称效应与少数族裔比例相关，即使在控制犯罪和收入后依然存在，但这种偏差伴随更高的地理知识水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益用于城市安全决策（如步行、租房、旅行），但其风险评估是否准确或存在系统性偏差尚不明确。本文旨在区分模型判断是基于实际地理风险还是社区名称引发的刻板印象，以评估其在决策支持中的可靠性和公平性。

Method: 对七个指令微调模型，在三种条件下（仅坐标、仅名称、名称+坐标）评估洛杉矶和芝加哥186个社区的安全评分，并与暴力犯罪率及ACS人口统计数据关联。采用线性回归分析名称效应与人口统计份额的关系，犯罪配对样本控制混杂因素，以及执法弹性分析区分报告犯罪与部署驱动犯罪。

Result: 1）六个模型在坐标条件下评分几乎平坦，名称承载大部分社区间差异且对暴力犯罪中度校准，仅前沿规模模型坐标通道有变化；2）名称降低安全评分对少数族裔比例高的社区更显著，且在所有模型和城市中稳健；3）洛杉矶中效果在控制犯罪和收入后仍存，并获配对样本证实；4）过度谨慎追踪近完全报告的凶杀而非选择性执法；5）地理知识更广的模型更倾向应用人口统计刻板印象。

Conclusion: 社区名称同时携带真实犯罪信号和人口统计刻板印象，移除名称虽减少偏差但也降低准确性。这凸显LLM在安全决策支持中的双重风险，需要谨慎设计输入以平衡准确性与公平性，并考虑部署时的社会影响。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to inform safety decisions in cities, such as where it is safe to walk, rent, or travel. We ask whether such judgments track measured risk or the patterns attached to an urban neighborhood's name. We probe seven instruct-tuned models under three conditions that dissociate name from geography: coordinates-only, name-only, and name+coordinates, across 186 neighborhoods in Los Angeles and Chicago, joined to violent crime and American Community Survey data. First, ratings are nearly flat under coordinates for six of seven models, while names carry most between neighborhood variation and are moderately calibrated to violent crime; only at frontier scale does the coordinate channel show appreciable variation. Second, names lower safety ratings more for neighborhoods with higher shares of the locally dominant marginalized group (percent Black in Chicago, percent Hispanic in Los Angeles), and this name effect tracks demographic share in all seven models and both cities. In Los Angeles, where demographic share and crime are more separable, the effect survives controls for crime and income and is confirmed by crime-matched pairs. An enforcement-elasticity analysis further shows that over-caution tracks near-fully-reported homicide rather than discretionary, deployment-driven offenses. Third, the effect scales with geographic knowledge: models that better distinguish real neighborhoods apply more demographic stereotype to them. Because neighborhood names carry both genuine crime signal and demographic stereotype, removing names reduces both bias and accuracy. We discuss implications for deploying LLMs in advice and decision-support settings.

</details>


### [172] [The Guard That Cried Wolf: How Scary Words Make Agent Guardrails Refuse Legitimate Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27009)
*Yingjie Zhang,Yuanbo Xie,Kai Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出首个衡量智能体护栏过度安全问题的基准Cautious Bench，包含756对可判定样本和40对不可判定样本，发现护栏存在"名称迷信"效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估忽视护栏过度安全问题，边界案例难以从真实数据收集且标签依赖人工判断，缺乏系统性基准。

Method: 通过设计样本与授权策略协同编码，利用构建时门控重新推导每个示例以机械验证标签，生成可判定良/恶样本对及不可判定样本。

Result: 测量五种设计的六种护栏，发现护栏在可怕名称下比良性名称更频繁拒绝授权操作，且仅名称变化导致偏差。

Conclusion: 护栏过度安全受表面名称影响而非授权上下文，基准为系统评估提供了可靠参照，暴露了现有护栏的认知偏差。

Abstract: Agent guardrails are checks that approve or refuse each action before an LLM executes it. Sometimes they refuse requests that are genuinely safe. This over-safety blocks deployment when a guardrail refuses an authorized task. Evaluating over-safety is hard: at the boundary an authorized action resembles an unauthorized one, and the safe-versus-unsafe label is a choice of authorization policy, not fixed by the action alone. We argue it therefore requires a benchmark that does not yet exist, one that maps the decision boundary of an ideal guardrail. Harvesting such a benchmark from real data is impractical: boundary cases are hard to collect, their labels hard to verify. The gap is real, so we construct Cautious Bench, the first benchmark to make over-safety the construct for agent guardrails; it codesigns each sample and its label with a stated authorization policy. A build-time gate re-derives every example to certify it, so each label is a mechanical consequence of the policy rather than an annotator's per-sample verdict, a reference against which researchers can measure real guardrails. The benchmark renders 756 Decidable benign/twin pairs, each under three object-name types (2,268 measured pairs), and 40 Undecidable pairs reported separately. Measuring six guardrails from five designs, we find a name-superstition effect: each over-refuses an authorized action more often under a scary-looking object name than a benign one. Since only the object name varies in the aforementioned contrast experiments, the deviation is the name's doing: the guardrails read the surface label, not the authorization context.

</details>


### [173] [Neighborhood Watch: Privacy Risks in Seeded Local Combination Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27037)
*Hadrien Lautraite,Tristan Allard,Anne-Sophie Charest,Jean-François Rajotte,Sébastien Gambs*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种局部组合合成数据生成方法（SMOTE、Simulant和Avatar）的隐私风险，通过成员推断、链接和重建攻击发现它们均存在显著的隐私泄露，质疑其输出在实践中是否应被视为匿名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据被视为敏感场景下共享数据的解决方案，但非差分隐私的方法仍存在重大残余风险，尤其是在医疗领域使用局部组合方法生成‘匿名化数据’时，需要评估其实际隐私保护能力。

Method: 针对SMOTE、Simulant和Avatar三种局部组合方法，实施多种隐私攻击（成员推断、链接和重建攻击），进行广泛隐私分析。

Result: 所有三种方法均显示出大量隐私泄露，攻击成功率较高，证实了严重的信息暴露风险。

Conclusion: 局部组合合成数据方法不能提供足够的隐私保障，其输出不应被视为匿名，需谨慎使用或结合更强的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Synthetic data is seen as a promising solution for sharing data in sensitive contexts. However, recent work on privacy attacks have shown that there are still significant residual risks, especially for synthetic data generations methods that are not based on formal approaches such as differential privacy. In this paper, we investigate the privacy risks associated with local combination approaches for generating synthetic data in which synthetic profiles are built by combining real neighbouring profiles. More precisely, we focus on three methods from this family, namely SMOTE, Simulant and Avatar, which have been recently used as a way to share 'anonymised data' in the healthcare domain. In particular, we conduct an extensive privacy analysis through a diverse set of attacks: membership inference, linkage and reconstruction attacks. Our results demonstrate substantial privacy leakage for all three methods, raising serious doubts about whether their outputs should be regarded as anonymous in practice.

</details>


### [174] [Cyber-Electromagnetic Anomaly Detection Through Time-Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27043)
*María Teresa Guillén Navarro,Juan Luis Serradilla Tormos,Sergio López Bernal,Daniel Díaz-López,Gregorio Martínez Pérez*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了两种融合电磁频谱与网络流量特征的异常检测模型（随机森林与LSTM自编码器），在ZBDS2023数据集上分别取得89.76%和64.09%的F1分数，验证了多域特征对网络态势感知的支撑作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法多聚焦于电磁或网络单一视角，难以刻画同时表现在两个域的事件，限制了网络态势感知中OODA环路的观测与解释能力。

Method: 利用ZBDS2023数据集（含物理层信号特征、流量层特征及标注攻击），构建两种模型：基于随机森林的监督学习模型和基于LSTM自编码器的无监督学习模型，联合两类特征进行异常检测。

Result: 随机森林模型F1分数达89.76%，显著优于LSTM自编码器的64.09%，表明监督学习模式在融合多域特征时更具优势。

Conclusion: 多域特征融合能提升网络态势感知的异常观测与解释能力，但正常与攻击样本间的细微差异表明仍需更丰富的判别性特征来进一步优化模型性能。

Abstract: Military operations benefit from the coordination between kinetic and non-kinetic domains. In particular, the coordination of cyber operations and electromagnetic warfare has become increasingly relevant for gaining operational advantage. This coordination is also relevant for Cyber Situational Awareness (CSA), where the Observe-Orient-Decide-Act (OODA) loop requires monitoring and interpreting evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this context, anomalies may appear not only in the physical behavior of signals, but also in the communication behavior observed at the traffic level. However, many existing anomaly detection proposals focus on only one of these perspectives, limiting their ability to characterize events that manifest simultaneously in the electromagnetic spectrum and cyberspace. To address this limitation, this work develops and evaluates two anomaly detection models that combine features from both domains. More specifically, the study uses the ZBDS2023 dataset, which contains traffic from nodes in a mesh network, including benign and attack behaviors. Thus, this dataset provides physical-level features, traffic-level features, and labeled attacks. Two detection approaches are evaluated: a supervised model based on Random Forest and an unsupervised model using LSTM-Autoencoder. The results show that learning-based models can detect patterns combining both levels, especially under a supervised approach, achieving an F1-score of 89.76% with Random Forest and 64.09% with LSTM-Autoencoder. Although these results indicate that the proposed models can support CSA by improving the observation and interpretation of anomalous behavior, the subtle differences between normal and attack samples highlight the need for richer discriminative features.

</details>


### [175] [The Framing Gap: Indirect Prompt-Injection Exfiltration Defeats Surface-Level Defenses in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27092)
*Md Habibur Rahman,Jaeho Kim*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 在安全合成实验中，工具型LLM代理面对间接提示注入时存在框架差距：相同泄露行为在直接命令下被拒绝（gpt-4o 0%），但重构成签名、配置字段或伪可信主机后成功率可达100%。攻击成本分三个层级，模板复用是核心资产。缓解措施中，目标白名单和能力隔离彻底关闭漏洞，而基于模型认知的防御（如SecAlign、通道分离、输出规范化）均失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示工具型LLM代理在面对攻击者控制的网页内容时，间接提示注入的脆弱性被系统性低估。现有防御多聚焦于模型对齐或策略强化，但缺乏对攻击重构（reframing）效果及其机制（指令/数据混淆）的量化分析，导致安全评估存在盲区。

Method: 构建安全合成环境（金丝雀秘密、模拟工具、干净vs污染对照指标），系统测试六种模型、十种直接注入类别及多种重构变体。进行三级成本分析（复述已知机制、替换模板字段、新建页面），并通过消融实验（移除保密策略）区分'指令/数据混淆'与'对齐失效'。评估多种防御：目标白名单、能力隔离（规划器/阅读器分离）、广泛策略子句、SecAlign微调、通道分离和输出规范化守卫。

Result: 直接注入被普遍拒绝（gpt-4o 0%），但重构后成功率升至100%。复述机制开销极低（3种措辞达96%），字段替换中等（60%），新机制创建几乎不可能（0/130）。消融显示机制为指令/数据混淆而非对齐失效。目标白名单和能力隔离均实现0%成功率；广泛策略子句有效（0%）但脆弱（删除后回升至48.8%）；SecAlign、通道分离和输出守卫均失效（32.5%、38.8%、ROT13下100%）。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性只能通过约束泄露目的地或隔离敏感能力获得，不能依赖执行模型自身识别攻击。防御应优先设计不可变的结构性限制（如白名单、能力分区），而非依赖语义理解或策略提示。现有公开防御（如SecAlign）在工具代理场景下无效，需重新评估其适用性。

Abstract: A tool-using LLM agent that reads attacker-controlled web content while holding a secret faces indirect prompt injection: the content may make it exfiltrate the secret. In a safe synthetic lab (canary secret, mock tools, matched clean-vs-poisoned metric) we report the framing gap: across six models, ten overt injection classes are refused (gpt-4o 0%), but reframing the identical leak as a mandatory integrity signature, config field, or look-alike "trusted" host drives gpt-4o 0% to 100%. The attack is cheap, and its cost is three-level: paraphrasing a known mechanism is trivial (96% at 3 wordings), swapping the field inside a known-effective template is also cheap (up to 60%), while authoring a fresh page around a new mechanism is hard (0/130) -- the reusable asset is the template, not the mechanism. An ablation shows the mechanism is instruction/data confusion, not defeated alignment: removing the confidentiality policy leaves base attacks at 0% and moves reframing only 31.9% to 38.1%. What closes the gap is payload-blind checks: a destination allow-list (0%, when destinations are closed) and a capability-isolating planner/reader split (0%). A broad "in any form" policy clause also closes it at the acting model (to 0%) but is brittle (dropping the catch-all reopens it to 48.8%). A published fine-tuning defense (SecAlign, CCS 2025) does not close it on a tool agent (32.5%, positive-control-validated), nor does channel separation (38.8%); an output-normalizing guard loses to a held-out encoding (ROT13, 100%). Robustness comes from constraining the destination or isolating the capability, not from the acting model recognizing the attack.

</details>


### [176] [SecureDrive-FL: Joint Differential Privacy and Gradient-Aware Selective Homomorphic Encryption for Federated Driver Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27108)
*Baran Can Gül,Hanuma Siddhartha Tunuguntla,Anjana Arvind Naik,Abhishek Vijay Potekar,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SecureDrive-FL框架，结合DP-SGD与GASHE选择性同态加密，在驾驶员监控联邦学习中同时抵御模型投毒和中间人攻击，性能接近纯DP-SGD且开销仅增8-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽保护原始数据，但梯度传输易被MitM窃取，且模型投毒破坏全局收敛；现有静态层级或全参数CKKS加密方案开销过高，缺乏与DP的协同设计。

Method: GASHE根据DP校准的敏感度阈值动态选择加密梯度分量，DP-SGD的噪声参数直接生成加密掩码，形成闭环DP+HE隐私管道，实现训练期隐私与通信期机密性统一。

Result: 在非IID十类驾驶员分心任务中，SecureDrive-FL投毒抵抗与DP-SGD相当（73.6% vs 74.0%准确率，ASR均3.9%），但抗MitM能力显著提升（78.2% vs 10.4%），额外运行时开销仅约8-10%。

Conclusion: 验证了选择性加密与DP协同的有效性，能以极小开销同时解决通信窃听和模型投毒威胁，为联邦学习隐私保护提供新范式。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-aware distributed training, yet gradient updates remain exploitable: Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) interception exposes updates in transit, while model poisoning corrupts global convergence. We first introduce GASHE (Gradient-Aware Selective Homomorphic Encryption), a novel selective encryption strategy that dynamically identifies and encrypts only the gradient components exceeding a DP-calibrated sensitivity threshold, rather than encrypting all parameters uniformly as in static layer-based or full-parameter CKKS schemes. Building on GASHE, we introduce SecureDrive-FL, a federated driver monitoring framework that couples DP-SGD with GASHE to create the first closed-loop DP+HE privacy pipeline: DP-SGD calibration parameters directly derive the GASHE encryption mask, unifying training-time privacy and communication-time confidentiality. Evaluated on a ten-class distracted driver classification task under non-IID federated splits, SecureDrive-FL matches DP-SGD alone's poisoning resistance (73.6% vs. 74.0% accuracy, 3.9% Attack Success Rate for both) while additionally withstanding MitM interception, where DP-SGD alone collapses to near-random accuracy (78.2% vs. 10.4%), all under only approx. 8--10% additional runtime overhead relative to DP-SGD alone---under DP-SGD noise injection with per-round privacy parameter epsilon_0=4.

</details>


### [177] [Safety Does Not Compose: Non-Decaying Loop State for Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27141)
*Chenhao Wu,Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Yingguang Yang,Yuhan Lin,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Yulin Huang,Jianshen Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Shang Luo,Kefu Xu,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文指出，现有大语言模型代理的安全防护仅基于单条轨迹，在多次迭代的代理循环中，这种设计会导致安全性失败。作者证明了轨迹级监控器在面对跨迭代碎片化攻击时，其真正例率等于假正例率，无法检测攻击。他们提出LoopHarness框架，通过维护跨迭代的持久安全状态，并在中介提交和仲裁检测下限条件下，将未授权不可逆操作的期望数量限制为常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理被部署为自主循环，从单个目标出发反复执行任务，但现有安全防护仅基于单条轨迹，在每次迭代开始时重置安全状态。这种设计在组合多个轨迹时存在根本缺陷，而不是实现细节问题。作者希望通过理论分析和实际框架解决跨迭代攻击的检测难题。

Method: 首先通过分离定理证明轨迹级监控器无法检测跨迭代碎片化证据。然后指出简单的几何衰减风险评分不足，因为冷却期不随范围N增长。接着提出LoopHarness，它在循环级别维护持久且非衰减的安全状态，结合中介提交和仲裁检测下限δ_M，并给出基于无模型规则的常数界限。最后设计了包含配对干净/攻击场景、跨迭代攻击套件、模块消融和自适应白盒红队的评估协议。

Result: LoopHarness将未授权不可逆操作的期望数量限制为B+m-1+m/δ_M，这是关于N的常数，其中B+m-1由无模型规则决定，即使在验证器完全合谋的情况下也能维持。实验表明，轨迹级监控器无法检测跨迭代攻击，而LoopHarness能有效区分攻击与正常行为。

Conclusion: 轨迹级安全防护在自主代理循环中本质上不充分，必须采用跨迭代的持久状态监控。LoopHarness提供了理论保证和实际验证，表明通过循环级安全状态可以有效限制攻击造成的损害，为自主代理的安全设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed as autonomous loops. Starting from one human goal, such a system repeatedly discovers work, plans, executes tool calls, verifies outcomes and persists state across many unattended iterations. The agent safeguards in wide use, however, are defined over a single trajectory, and their safety state is re-initialized when the next trajectory begins. We show that this is a failure of composition rather than an implementation detail. Our central result is a separation: against an attack whose evidence is fragmented across several iterations, every trajectory-scoped monitor has a true-positive rate equal to its false-positive rate, however expressive it is, because the evidence it would need never appears in the window it sees, whereas a monitor retaining cross-iteration state separates the two perfectly. We further show that the obvious repair of carrying a geometrically decaying risk score is insufficient, because the cooling-off period a patient adversary must wait is a constant that does not grow with the horizon $N$. We then present LoopHarness, which restores a persistent, non-decaying safety state at the loop level. Under mediated commits and an arbiter detection floor $δ_M$, it bounds the expected number of unauthorized irreversible actions by $B+m-1+m/δ_M$, a constant in $N$, of which the $B+m-1$ term is decided by a model-free rule and therefore survives a fully colluding verifier. We give a complete evaluation protocol on native Agent-SafetyBench tasks with paired clean and attacked episodes, an outer-state attack suite whose decisive evidence exists only across iterations, per-module ablations, and an adaptive white-box red team.

</details>


### [178] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出J-Zero框架，通过挑战者-求解者-判别者协同进化，在无需人类标注的偏好对下，同时提升可验证与不可验证领域的自进化能力，且在十次迭代中持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法在可验证领域表现良好，但不可验证领域因缺乏客观奖励信号而进展有限，且已有方法在多次迭代中易性能退化。

Method: 采用挑战者与求解者对抗式协同进化，挑战者生成难度递增的任务，求解者提升回答质量；判别者利用已知顺序的偏好对（非自身得分）进行适应，包括求解者答案对挑战者答案、分解重组答案对一次性答案。

Result: 在可验证领域平均提升4.2分，不可验证领域提升8.0分，且十次迭代后持续进步，而基线在两次迭代后即退化。

Conclusion: J-Zero有效解决不可验证领域的自进化难题，通过无监督偏好对和协同进化机制实现稳定且持续的模型提升，为通用自进化系统提供新路径。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [179] [X-WAD: eXplainable Web Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27172)
*Matteo Bitussi,Roberto Doriguzzi-Corin*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Transformer语言模型（TLMs）的HTTP请求异常检测，利用token级logit惊奇度映射提供异常分数和热力图解释，并发现公开数据集中的标签不一致导致模型出现后门式失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网络环境中良性流量占主导，防御系统常采用半监督方法仅用正常数据训练。但训练数据难以保证完全无异常，污染样本会引入后门，使模型将某些攻击静默误判为正常。现有可解释性研究不足，亟需在提供检测的同时给出细粒度解释。

Method: 采用Transformer语言模型（TLMs）建模HTTP请求的token序列，通过token级logit计算惊奇度（surprisal），融合得到整体异常分数，并用热力图突出异常token，实现逐token的可解释异常定位。

Result: 在公开数据集上验证了方法的有效性，成功发现训练数据中的标签不一致问题，证明异常污染引发的后门式缺陷可被该解释方法揭示。

Conclusion: 所提token级惊奇度解释方法不仅能有效检测HTTP请求异常，还能为检测结果提供直接解释，帮助识别并纠正训练数据中的污染问题，提升防御系统的可靠性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of web-based services, particularly API-driven architectures, reflects an increasing reliance on distributed systems, exposing sensitive data to security risks and making the adoption of automated defensive mechanisms essential. In this context, where benign traffic predominates in real-world settings, modern defenses increasingly model normal behavior, relying on semi-supervised approaches trained on only normal data. However, ensuring the complete absence of anomalous instances in such training data is inherently difficult in practice, and mislabeled or contaminated attack samples can introduce backdoors into the learned defense, causing the model to silently misclassify certain attack patterns as normal behavior. This paper investigates the effectiveness of Transformer-based Language Models (TLMs) in the detection of anomalies in HTTP requests, focussing on providing detailed explanations for the detected anomalies. The study employs token-level logit-based surprisal mapping to provide both an anomaly score and a direct, detailed explanation via heatmap-like highlighting. The effectiveness of the proposed explainability approach is demonstrated by the discovery of labelling inconsistencies in a popular public dataset, revealing how anomalous contamination in the training data had induced backdoor-like failures in the detection models.

</details>


### [180] [From Security Events to Conflict States: A Three-layer Cyber Defense Scenario Model for Enhanced Cyber Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27215)
*Miguel Requena Micó,Mario Fernandez-Tarraga,Daniel Díaz-López,Sergio López Bernal,Gregorio Martínez Pérez*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一个面向任务的网络防御框架，通过三层概率模型统一表示攻击进程、观测不确定性、任务影响和防御决策，并在NetLogo中实现可执行仿真，验证了其数学一致性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务关键环境中的网络防御需要集成方法，以统一框架表示攻击进展、防御方不确定性、任务影响和防御决策支持，尤其是在不完整和噪声观测下持续评估安全态势并保持任务连续性。

Method: 构建三层集成概率模型：(1)攻击图模型表示攻击进展；(2)事件模型通过贝叶斯推断将遥测转换为后验信念；(3)状态模型将推断态势抽象为冲突状态和任务风险等级；并连接单步防御行动规则，平衡残余任务风险与运营成本；使用NetLogo进行代理仿真，涵盖TEZ、MOZ和ESZ区域。

Result: 通过数学一致性分析、遥测扰动局部鲁棒性评估及代表性仿真轨迹，表明框架保持了攻击进展、遥测驱动不确定性管理、任务影响评估与成本感知防御决策之间的连贯关系。

Conclusion: 该框架及其实现有效支持以任务风险优先的态势感知和决策辅助，适用于任务关键环境的网络防御。

Abstract: Cyber defense in mission-critical environments requires integrated approaches capable of representing adversarial progression, defender-side uncertainty, mission impact, and defensive decision support within a unified framework. In operational domains, defenders must continuously estimate the evolving security posture while preserving the continuity and integrity of mission-critical functions under incomplete and noisy observations. This paper presents a mission-oriented cyber-defense framework for Cyber Situational Awareness (CSA) and decision support based on a three-layer integrated probabilistic model and an executable simulation prototype. The model combines: (i) an attack-graph model that represents possible adversarial progression through mission-relevant assets, (ii) an event model that transforms observed telemetry into posterior defender beliefs through Bayesian inference, and (iii) a state model that abstracts the inferred posture into conflict states and mission-risk levels. These components are connected to a one-step defensive action rule that balances estimated residual mission risk and operational cost. The framework is instantiated in a NetLogo agent-based simulation of an operational environment structured across the Tactical Edge Zone (TEZ), Mission Operations Zone (MOZ), and Enterprise Support Zone (ESZ). The proposal is assessed through mathematical consistency analysis, local robustness assessment under telemetry perturbations, and representative simulation traces. Results indicate that the framework and its implementation preserve coherent relationships between attack progression, telemetry-driven uncertainty management, mission-impact assessment, and cost-aware defensive decision support guided by mission-risk prioritization.

</details>


### [181] [SPA: Securing Persistent LLM Agents Across Queries with Plan-First Information-Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27234)
*Dylan Girrens,Guangjing Wang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SPA架构，通过计划优先执行、双格信息流控制和标签化持久化，将工具知识攻击成功率降至0（AgentDojo）和0.2%（AgentDojo-MQ），实现持久化LLM代理的安全增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅保护规划或单次工具交互，无法应对持久代理中攻击者数据篡改控制流、污染安全敏感参数或危害后续查询的跨查询威胁。

Method: SPA采用计划优先架构，每次查询由规划器生成完整可执行DSL计划，实施双格信息流控制以追踪显式数据流和控制依赖的机密性与完整性，持久化时仅存储带标签工件并只向后续规划暴露语义元数据，避免重新暴露不可信载荷。

Result: 在tool_knowledge攻击下，SPA使AgentDojo攻击成功率为零，AgentDojo-MQ上降至0.2%，验证了标签持久化对延迟攻击的抑制作用。

Conclusion: 计划优先执行与标签保持持久化可显著强化持久化LLM代理，但严格完整性控制会引入安全-效用权衡，提示需在安全性与任务能力间取得平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over untrusted webpages, documents, tools, and persistent states while exercising authority over security-sensitive resources. Existing defenses typically protect either planning or individual tool interactions, but persistent agents face a broader threat: attacker-controlled data can alter control flow, enter security-sensitive tool arguments, or compromise later queries. We present SPA, a plan-first architecture that secures planning, execution, and cross-query state reuse. SPA invokes the planner once per query to generate a complete executable plan in a declarative domain-specific language, then applies dual-lattice information-flow control to track confidentiality and integrity across explicit data flows and control dependencies. To support persistence without re-exposing untrusted payloads to the planner, SPA stores execution results as labeled artifacts and reveals only semantic metadata during later planning. We evaluate SPA on AgentDojo and AgentDojo-MQ, which is our multi-query extension for measuring secure state reuse and delayed attacks. Under the 'tool_knowledge' attack, SPA with information-flow control reduces attack success to zero on AgentDojo and 0.2% on AgentDojo-MQ. Our results show that plan-first execution combined with label-preserving persistence can substantially strengthen persistent LLM agents, while revealing an important security-utility tradeoff introduced by strict integrity enforcement.

</details>


### [182] [Robust Neural Stimulation Response Modeling Through Meta-Learning and Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26649)
*Matthew J Bryan,Daniel C Muir,Felix Schwock,Azadeh Yazdan-Shahmorad,Rajesh P N Rao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次证明元学习和预训练可用于神经刺激响应建模，通过跨会话预训练将预测灾难性失败从16/40降至1/40，并将校准需求减少50-90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动的闭环神经刺激在临床应用中受两大阻碍：预测模型在相当比例会话中灾难性失败，以及逐会话校准耗时与临床约束不兼容。

Method: 在时间基函数模型（TBFM）基础上，提出基于模型无关元学习（MAML）的新型架构与算法，实现跨会话预训练，并在两只非人灵长类初级感觉运动皮层40次光遗传刺激会话中评估。

Result: MAML预训练显著降低灾难性预测失败：1k校准集下R²<0.05的会话从16/40降至1/40；预测区间显著变窄（p<0.05）；在匹配精度下校准需求减少50-90%。

Conclusion: 刺激响应的跨会话结构足够稳定以支持预训练，元学习在神经刺激中可行且有效，为部署模型驱动的闭环刺激控制器扫除关键障碍，并激励构建标准化多站点刺激数据集与进一步探索稳健闭环刺激元学习。

Abstract: Objective: Model-based closed-loop neural stimulation holds promise for therapeutic applications ranging from Parkinson's disease to sensory restoration, but deployment has been limited by two obstacles: 1) forecasting models for predicting the consequences of stimulation fail catastrophically on a meaningful fraction of sessions, and 2) per-session calibration requirements are often incompatible with clinical constraints. We address both by demonstrating, for the first time, that meta-learning and pretraining can be applied to neural stimulation response modeling. Methods: Temporal basis function models (TBFMs) forecast state-dependent neural responses to stimulation. We extend TBFMs with cross-session pretraining using a novel architecture and algorithm based on model-agnostic meta-learning (MAML), evaluating them on 40 sessions of optogenetic stimulation in primary sensorimotor cortex of two non-human primates. Results: Meta-learning substantially reduces catastrophic forecast failure: for a 1k calibration set size, sessions with test R-squared < 0.05 drop from 16 of 40 (single-session training) to 1 (MAML-pretrained), and prediction intervals become significantly narrower (p < 0.05). Calibration requirements are reduced by 50-90% at matched accuracy, enabling experiments otherwise infeasible within clinical session-time constraints. Conclusion: Our results demonstrate that cross-session structure in stimulation responses is consistent enough to support pretraining, providing the first empirical evidence that meta-learning approaches are viable for neural stimulation. Significance: The robustness and sample efficiency gains directly address known obstacles to deploying model-based stimulation controllers. Our results motivate community efforts to assemble standardized multi-site stimulation datasets and to further explore meta-learning for robust closed-loop stimulation.

</details>


### [183] [Low-ASR Backdoors: Exploiting Attack Success Rate Reduction and Attacker-Defender Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27288)
*Arham Riaz,Ting Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文揭示后门攻击中高攻击成功率(ASR)并非后门的固有属性，而是攻击者可控变量；通过反向训练框架可生成低ASR但保留后门行为的模型，使现有防御全面失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击与防御均假设成功后门需具备高ASR，但该假设存在根本性缺陷——ASR可被攻击者主动降低而不消除后门行为，导致防御范式的盲区。

Method: 提出反向训练框架，通过弱化触发器与目标标签的关联来训练模型，在保持干净输入性能的同时显著降低ASR，从而规避依赖高ASR信号的防御机制。

Result: 在多个数据集、多种攻击家族及多种架构下，现有最先进防御方法在低ASR条件下持续失效，验证了攻击者与防御者之间的根本不对称性。

Conclusion: 研究表明ASR作为后门检测指标不可靠，攻击者可通过控制ASR轻松绕过防御，需重新设计不依赖ASR假设的后门防御机制。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks are among the most effective and stealthy attacks in deep learning. Existing attacks and defenses are largely designed and evaluated under the assumption that successful backdoors exhibit high Attack Success Rates (ASRs). In this paper, we show that this assumption creates a fundamental weakness in existing defense paradigms. ASR is not an intrinsic property of a backdoor; rather, it is an attacker-controlled variable that can be deliberately reduced without eliminating the underlying backdoor behavior. We introduce a reverse-training framework that weakens the trigger-target association, producing low-ASR backdoor models while preserving clean-input performance. Through extensive evaluation across multiple datasets, diverse attack families, and multiple architectures, we show that state-of-the-art defenses fail consistently under low-ASR conditions, exposing a fundamental attacker-defender asymmetry.

</details>


### [184] [When Context Gets Root: Privilege Escalation in LLM Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27299)
*Xingbang He,Yuanwei Chen,Yi Qian,Haiyang Wei,Ligeng Chen,Zenan Fu,Linzhang Wang,Hao Wu,Bing Mao*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种针对AI代理的指令权限提升攻击，利用代理框架对指令级别的重构，将低权限内容提升为高权限指令，从而绕过模型侧防御，在多种代理框架上实现广泛攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令层级防御假设指令来源决定权限，但代理框架在每次模型调用时构建上下文，可能将低层级内容提升至更高指令层级，赋予其更大权限，从而绕过现有防御。

Method: 通过多代理机制，在六个编码代理框架上实现13种攻击目标（涵盖机密性、完整性、可用性和远程代码执行），验证在无限制操作和自动权限审查两种场景下的攻击效果，并利用代理提供的持久目标和计划任务复现漏洞。

Result: 在无限制操作下，所有六个框架均达成全部13个目标；在自动权限审查下，所有三个提供该模式的框架也全部达成目标，证明攻击具有普遍性。

Conclusion: 指令权限提升是通用且严重的代理安全威胁，现有指令层级防御无法有效防护，需重新设计代理上下文构建与权限管理机制。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy is a model-side defense that assigns instructions different levels of privilege according to their sources. These levels constrain which content may direct model behavior. During agent execution, however, agent harnesses construct context for each model invocation. This construction can elevate low-level content to a higher instruction level and grant it greater model-facing privilege. We introduce instruction privilege escalation. In this attack, an attacker induces an agent to elevate low-level malicious content to a higher instruction level. The elevated content then causes the agent to execute instructions it would not follow at their original level. We evaluate this threat by using multi-agent mechanisms to achieve 13 attack objectives across six coding-agent harnesses. These objectives span confidentiality, integrity, availability, and remote code execution. With unrestricted action execution, the attacks achieve all 13 objectives on all six harnesses. Under automatic permission review, the attacks achieve all 13 objectives on all three harnesses that provide this mode. We further reproduce the vulnerability using harness-provided persistent goals and scheduled tasks. These results demonstrate the generality of instruction privilege escalation.

</details>


### [185] [SKILL.state: Scalable Long-Horizon Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26263)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Jonghyun Chung*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SKILL.state运行时架构，用显式可变执行状态替代追加式对话历史，仅保留技能规范、当前状态和最新观察，丢弃中间推理，从而提升长周期任务准确率并大幅降低累计token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体运行时通过不断追加对话历史来维持执行，导致延迟增加和上下文污染，尤其在长周期任务中表现严重，需要更高效的执行状态管理机制。

Method: 设计SKILL.state架构，每个执行步骤仅向模型提供不可变技能规范、当前结构化执行状态和最新观察；中间推理在生成有效状态更新后立即丢弃，阻断提示词随历史增长。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和执行环境下，SKILL.state相比基线提升任务准确率，同时显著降低累计token消耗，验证显式执行状态的有效性和架构无关性。

Conclusion: 显式执行状态是可扩展的长周期智能体技能的有效抽象，为未来智能体运行时设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly act as autonomous agents executing complex, long-running procedural skills. Existing agent runtimes maintain execution by continually appending observations, actions, and intermediate reasoning traces to an ever-growing conversation history, causing latency degradation and context-poisoning failures over long horizons. We present SKILL.state, a runtime architecture that replaces append-only conversational history with an explicit, mutable execution state. At each execution step, the model receives only the immutable skill specification, the current structured execution state, and the latest observation. Intermediate reasoning is discarded immediately after producing a validated state update, preventing prompt growth with execution history. Across diverse datasets, models, and execution environments, SKILL.state improves task accuracy while substantially reducing cumulative token consumption. Our results demonstrate that explicit execution state is an effective and architecture-agnostic abstraction for scalable long-horizon agent skills.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond F1: Evaluating Coverage and Failure Recovery in AI Model Security Scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27424)
*Qianlong Lan,Vinothini Pandurangan,Anuj Kaul,Indranil Sanyal*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 现有静态扫描器评估混淆了判断可用性与判断准确性，通过受控基准测试发现ModelAudit覆盖最全，ModelScan条件精度虽高但覆盖率低，并强调需区分增量检测覆盖与工具冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规评估指标仅刻画扫描器能给出安全判断的情况，忽略了无法给出判断（如分析失败或未覆盖）的情形，导致对工具实际效用的误判。

Method: 构建包含170个Pickle和PyTorch相关工件的合成基准（145个家族，135个有二元安全真值，10个无标签但故意畸形），显式区分覆盖率、分析完成度、确定性安全判断、非安全发现和不支持结果，评估ModelScan、ModelAudit和Fickling三类工具。

Result: ModelAudit对135个有标签家族全部给出确定性判断（100%），Fickling为81.5%，ModelScan仅49.6%；但ModelScan条件判断下精确率、召回率和F1均为100%。Fickling未发现超出ModelAudit+ModelScan组合的独有真阳家族。ModelScan失败的48个恶意家族中，ModelAudit和Fickling均产生与真值一致的检测。

Conclusion: 评估需分离判断可用性与判断准确性，并区分增量检测覆盖率与工具级冗余；选择扫描器时应优先考虑覆盖率，同时警惕工具间重复检测而非互补价值。

Abstract: Static scanners are increasingly used to identify executable or otherwise unsafe content in machine- learning artifacts, yet conventional evaluation metrics characterize only cases where a scanner yields a usable security judgment. We evaluate ModelScan, ModelAudit, and Fickling using a controlled, artifact-backed benchmark on a synthetic corpus of 170 Pickle and PyTorch focused artifacts across 145 specimen families, 135 of which have binary security ground truth and 10 of which are intentionally malformed without labels. We explicitly distinguish non-N/A coverage, analysis completion, definitive security decisions, non-security findings, and unsupported outcomes. On labeled families, ModelAudit produced definitive security decisions for all 135 families (100%), Fickling for 110 (81.5%), and ModelScan for 67 (49.6%). Conditional on making a definitive judgment, ModelScan achieved 100% precision, recall, and F1. Fickling identified no unique true- positive families beyond those found by the combination of ModelAudit and ModelScan. Furthermore, for the 48 malicious families where ModelScan failed to complete its analysis, both ModelAudit and Fickling generated detections consistent with ground truth. These findings underscore the need to separate judgment accuracy from judgment availability, as well as incremental detection coverage from tool-level redundancy.

</details>


### [187] [RedEvoAgent: Automatic Red-Teaming Agent with Experience-Driven Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27439)
*Junjie Zhang,Hui Liu,Kecheng Chen,Xianbo Mo,Changsheng Chen,Haoliang Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RedEvoAgent，一种将跨案例攻击轨迹蒸馏为简洁可读攻击技能的黑盒红队智能体，通过工具有效性分析和决策式工具归因实现技能自适应进化，并利用验证棘轮保留有效更新，在多个基准上超越固定攻击和现有智能体基线，同时提升工具效率并具备跨模型和跨环境迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在真实执行环境中面临越狱攻击引发有害工具调用和持久状态改变的风险；自动红队方法多依赖固定攻击，而最新智能体攻击者虽通过轨迹检索协调多个越狱工具，但存在检索偏差、工具归因不清、完整轨迹带来上下文开销和可解释性下降的问题。

Method: RedEvoAgent将跨案例攻击轨迹蒸馏为简洁的人类可读攻击技能，通过工具有效性分析（Tool-Effectiveness Profiling）和决策工具归因（Deciding-Tool Attribution）更新技能，并引入验证棘轮（Validation Ratchet）机制，仅保留能提升验证性能的更新，以自适应进化攻击策略。

Result: 在多个基准、目标模型和目标执行环境上的实验表明，RedEvoAgent优于固定攻击基线和现有智能体攻击基线，提高了工具使用效率，并且攻击技能可跨攻击者模型和目标执行环境迁移。

Conclusion: 通过将轨迹蒸馏为可解释技能并加入自适应更新和验证门槛，能更高效、更可靠地实现黑盒环境下的自动化红队攻击，同时减少上下文开销并提升可迁移性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in product-level execution harnesses, where jailbreaks can trigger harmful tool use and persistent state changes, creating greater risks than unsafe text generation alone. Existing automatic red-teaming methods often rely on fixed attacks, while recent agentic attackers coordinate multiple jailbreak tools and show stronger potential through trajectory-based retrieval. However, such retrieval can reuse misleading experiences due to retrieval bias and unclear tool credit, and full trajectories add context overhead while reducing interpretability. We propose RedEvoAgent, a black-box red-teaming agent that distills cross-case attack trajectories into a concise, human-readable attack skill. The attack skill adaptively evolves through tool-effectiveness profiling and Deciding-Tool Attribution for skill updates, and a validation ratchet that retains only updates improving validation performance. Experiments on multiple benchmarks, target models, and target execution harnesses show that RedEvoAgent outperforms fixed and agentic baselines, improves tool efficiency, and transfers across attacker models and target execution harnesses.

</details>


### [188] [Rethinking Message Passing as Retrieval for Text-Attributed Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26732)
*Jintang Li,Yuhong Chen,Ruofan Wu,Binli Luo,Jiayi Ji,Hui Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出检索增强视角下的GNN，并基于此设计RTA框架，用标签感知检索与传播替代结构消息传递，在多个文本属性图基准上匹敌或超越强基线，同时提升效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在实践中表现优异，但其成功原因尚不清楚，且存在计算开销大、对图结构敏感的问题。本文旨在从检索增强角度重新解释GNN，并开发更高效、鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: RTA：一个简单的MLP框架，将结构消息传递替换为标签感知的检索与传播。每层通过MLP处理节点表示与检索上下文的置换不变汇总；理论上证明检索聚合与softmax注意力消息传递的联系，并分析检索上下文监督对错误检索的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个文本属性图基准上，RTA匹配或超越强GNN和图LLM基线，同时在不同场景下展现出更高的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 检索增强视角为理解GNN提供新思路，RTA验证了无需显式消息传递也能达到优异性能，且更高效鲁棒，为图学习提供更轻量有效的方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are typically conceptualized as message-passing neural networks, yet it remains unclear why neighborhood aggregation reliably outperforms node-wise multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). Despite its empirical success, this paradigm can be computationally expensive and sensitive to imperfect graph structures. In this work, we present a retrieval-augmented view of GNNs: each layer makes predictions by applying an MLP to a node representation together with a permutation-invariant summary of retrieved graph context. Motivated by this perspective, we propose RTA, a simple MLP-based framework that replaces structural message passing with label-aware retrieval and propagation. We provide theoretical insights that (i) connect retrieval-based aggregation to softmax-attention message passing, and (ii) establish the robustness of retrieved-context supervision to mis-retrieved outliers. Experiments on multiple text-attributed graph benchmarks show that RTA matches or even outperforms strong GNN and graph LLM baselines while improving efficiency and robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [189] [Safety by Design: Realized-Cost Constraints for Contextual Bandits with Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26755)
*Spyros Dragazis,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种面向异方差环境下、对实际成本施加逐阶段高概率约束的上下文赌博机算法HPC-UCB，在保障安全的同时实现紧致遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全约束赌博机模型仅约束期望成本，在异方差场景下无法控制单次决策的实际成本风险，可能导致灾难性后果。

Method: 采用乐观-悲观策略：乐观估计奖励以促进探索，悲观估计安全动作集以保守满足高概率成本约束；对线性模型证明紧致遗憾界，并推广至一般函数类。
